// Rule Element Count: 28
rule("loading pause")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        LoadingPause;
    }

    // Element Count: 27, Action Count: 6
    // Largest Action Index: 2 using 12 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
        If(Compare(Global Variable(start), ==, False));
            Wait Until(And(Compare(Server Load, <, 180), Compare(Server Load Average, <, 180)), 10);
        Else;
            Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 200), 2);
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 197
rule("decode text list")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        decompressTextSub;
    }

    // Element Count: 196, Action Count: 35
    // Largest Action Index: 20 using 49 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Global Variable(decodeOut, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(textGroup, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(textItem, Custom String(""));
        Set Global Variable(decodeString, 0);
        While(Compare(Global Variable(decodeString), <, Count Of(Global Variable(decodeIn))));
            Set Global Variable(decodePosition, 0);
            While(Compare(Global Variable(decodePosition), <, String Length(Value In Array(Global Variable(decodeIn), Global Variable(decodeString)))));
                Set Global Variable(textToken, Index Of String Char(Global Variable(decodeTable), String Slice(Value In Array(Global Variable(decodeIn), Global Variable(decodeString)), Global Variable(decodePosition), 1)));
                If(Compare(Global Variable(textToken), ==, 0));
                    Set Global Variable At Index(textGroup, Count Of(Global Variable(textGroup)), If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(textItem), ==, Custom String("")), Null, String Slice(Global Variable(textItem), 0, String Length(Global Variable(textItem)))));
                    Set Global Variable(textItem, Custom String(""));
                    If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 53), ==, True));
                        Set Global Variable At Index(infoText, Subtract(Count Of(Global Variable(textGroup)), 1), Last Of(Global Variable(textGroup)));
                        Wait(0.15, Ignore Condition);
                    End;
                Else If(Compare(Global Variable(textToken), ==, 1));
                    Set Global Variable At Index(decodeOut, Count Of(Global Variable(decodeOut)), Global Variable(textGroup));
                    Set Global Variable(textGroup, Empty Array);
                Else;
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 51, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 49), Round To Integer(Divide(Global Variable(textToken), 62), Down)));
                    Set Global Variable(textItem, Custom String("{0}{1}", Global Variable(textItem), String Slice(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 51), Multiply(Modulo(Global Variable(textToken), 62), 2), If-Then-Else(Compare(String Slice(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 51), Add(Multiply(Modulo(Global Variable(textToken), 62), 2), 1), 1), ==, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 50)), 1, 2))));
                    If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 53), ==, True));
                        Set Global Variable At Index(infoText, Count Of(Global Variable(textGroup)), Global Variable(textItem));
                    End;
                End;
                If(Not(Modulo(Global Variable(decodePosition), 12)));
                    Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                End;
                Modify Global Variable(decodePosition, Add, 1);
            End;
            Modify Global Variable(decodeString, Add, 1);
        End;
        If(Compare(Count Of(Global Variable(decodeOut)), ==, 0));
            Set Global Variable(decodeOut, Global Variable(textGroup));
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 119
rule("start hero counter damage")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        StartCounterMods;
    }

    // Element Count: 118, Action Count: 13
    // Largest Action Index: 5 using 44 Elements
    actions
    {
        For Player Variable(Event Player, counterSlot, 0, Count Of(Player Variable(Event Player, counterMods)), 1);
            Stop Damage Modification(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, counterMods), Player Variable(Event Player, counterSlot)));
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, counterMods, Empty Array);
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 16), ==, True));
            Start Damage Modification(All Players(Team 2), Event Player, If-Then-Else(Compare(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 11), Add(Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(counterHeroType), Index Of Array Value(Global Variable(hero), Hero Of(Players In Slot(Subtract(Slot Of(Event Player), 4), Team 1)))), 3), Value In Array(Global Variable(counterHeroType), Index Of Array Value(Global Variable(hero), Hero Of(Event Player))))), >, 100), 150, 100), Receivers Damagers and Damage Percent);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, counterMods, Append To Array, Last Damage Modification ID);
        Else;
            For Player Variable(Event Player, counterSlot, 0, 3, 1);
                Start Damage Modification(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 2), Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(counterHeroType), Index Of Array Value(Global Variable(hero), Hero Of(Current Array Element))), ==, Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, counterSlot)))), Event Player, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 11), Add(Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(counterHeroType), Index Of Array Value(Global Variable(hero), Hero Of(Event Player))), 3), Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, counterSlot)))), Receivers Damagers and Damage Percent);
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, counterMods, Append To Array, Last Damage Modification ID);
            End;
        End;
    }
}

    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 29
rule("global bank interest gain every round")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 18, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(numberOfZombies) <= 0;
        Global Variable(bankPoints) > 0;
        Global Variable(start) == 1;
    }

    // Element Count: 10, Action Count: 2
    // Largest Action Index: 1 using 8 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait(0.16, Ignore Condition);
        Modify Global Variable(bankPoints, Add, Multiply(Global Variable(bankPoints), Value In Array(Global Variable(bankTiers_InterestRate), Global Variable(bankTier))));
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 79
rule("global bank tier upgrade")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 31, Condition Count: 4
    conditions
    {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Distance Between(Event Player, First Of(Global Variable(bankButtons))) < 1;
        Global Variable(bankTier) < Subtract(Count Of(Global Variable(bankTiers_Cost)), 1);
    }

    // Element Count: 47, Action Count: 8
    // Largest Action Index: 0 using 12 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(Compare(Global Variable(bankPoints), >=, Value In Array(Global Variable(bankTiers_Cost), Add(Global Variable(bankTier), 1))));
            Modify Global Variable(bankPoints, Subtract, Value In Array(Global Variable(bankTiers_Cost), Add(Global Variable(bankTier), 1)));
            Modify Global Variable(bankTier, Add, 1);
            Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("You have permanently upgraded your bank to tier {0}", Global Variable(bankTier)));
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Buff Explosion Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70);
        Else;
            Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("You need {0} points in the bank to upgrade!", Value In Array(Global Variable(bankTiers_Cost), Add(Global Variable(bankTier), 1))));
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 91
rule("global bank withdraw button")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 23, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global Variable(bankButtons), 1)) < 1;
    }

    // Element Count: 67, Action Count: 14
    // Largest Action Index: 3 using 29 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, bankHoldStart, Total Time Elapsed);
        While(Compare(Global Variable(bankPoints), >, 0));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, bankStep, Min(If-Then-Else(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, Player Variable(Event Player, bankHoldStart)), <, 2), 250, If-Then-Else(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, Player Variable(Event Player, bankHoldStart)), <, 5), 1000, 10000)), Global Variable(bankPoints)));
            Modify Global Variable(bankPoints, Subtract, Player Variable(Event Player, bankStep));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Add, Player Variable(Event Player, bankStep));
            Stop Chasing Player Variable(Event Player, points2);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, points2, Player Variable(Event Player, points));
            If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, withdrawNotif), ==, 0));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, withdrawNotif, 1);
            End;
            Wait(0.25, Abort When False);
        End;
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("The bank is out of funds!"));
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 93
rule("global bank deposit button")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 23, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global Variable(bankButtons), 2)) < 1;
    }

    // Element Count: 69, Action Count: 14
    // Largest Action Index: 3 using 30 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, bankHoldStart, Total Time Elapsed);
        While(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, points), >, 0));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, bankStep, Min(If-Then-Else(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, Player Variable(Event Player, bankHoldStart)), <, 2), 200, If-Then-Else(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, Player Variable(Event Player, bankHoldStart)), <, 5), 1000, 10000)), Player Variable(Event Player, points)));
            Modify Global Variable(bankPoints, Add, Player Variable(Event Player, bankStep));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Player Variable(Event Player, bankStep));
            Stop Chasing Player Variable(Event Player, points2);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, points2, Player Variable(Event Player, points));
            If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, depNotif), ==, 0));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, depNotif, 1);
            End;
            Wait(0.25, Abort When False);
        End;
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You don't have enough points to deposit!"));
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 120
rule("credit card system")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 34, Condition Count: 4
    conditions
    {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global Variable(bankButtons), 4)) < 1;
        Player Variable(Event Player, creditTier) < Subtract(Count Of(Global Variable(creditCardTiers_Cost)), 1);
    }

    // Element Count: 85, Action Count: 15
    // Largest Action Index: 1 using 14 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, points), >=, Value In Array(Global Variable(creditCardTiers_Cost), Add(Player Variable(Event Player, creditTier), 1))));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Value In Array(Global Variable(creditCardTiers_Cost), Add(Player Variable(Event Player, creditTier), 1)));
            Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, points)), 1000, Destination And Rate);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, creditTier, Add, 1);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, creditcardlimit, Value In Array(Global Variable(creditCardTiers_Limit), Player Variable(Event Player, creditTier)));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, cashback, Value In Array(Global Variable(creditCardTiers_Cashback), Player Variable(Event Player, creditTier)));
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Your credit card is now tier {0}", Player Variable(Event Player, creditTier)));
            Wait(4, Ignore Condition);
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Hold spacebar to see credit card details"));
        Else If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, creditTier), ==, 0));
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You need {0} points buy a credit card!", Value In Array(Global Variable(creditCardTiers_Cost), 1)));
        Else;
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You need {0} points to upgrade!", Value In Array(Global Variable(creditCardTiers_Cost), Add(Player Variable(Event Player, creditTier), 1))));
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 55
rule("credit card interest")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 25, Condition Count: 4
    conditions
    {
        Player Variable(Event Player, points) < 0;
        Global Variable(numberOfZombies) < 1;
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Player Variable(Event Player, creditTier) > 0;
    }

    // Element Count: 29, Action Count: 4
    // Largest Action Index: 0 using 11 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 48, Multiply(Player Variable(Event Player, points), Value In Array(Global Variable(creditCardTiers_InterestRate), Player Variable(Event Player, creditTier))));
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Add, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 48));
        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, points)), 1000, Destination And Rate);
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You have incurred {0} interest on your credit card.", Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 48)));
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 179
rule("2 round change")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 26, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(numberOfZombies) <= 0;
        Global Variable(start) == 1;
        Is True For Any(All Players(Team 1), Compare(Player Variable(Current Array Element, outofpsawn), ==, 1)) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 152, Action Count: 36
    // Largest Action Index: 6 using 12 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait(0.16, Ignore Condition);
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(omnicspawn)), 1);
            Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Value In Array(Global Variable(omnicspawn), Global Variable(forindex)));
        End;
        Modify Global Variable(roundnumber, Add, 1);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 28, 1);
        Big Message(All Players(All Teams), String("round {0}", Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 62), Global Variable(romanNcounter))));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 23, False);
        Set Global Variable(roundRespawns, Filtered Array(All Dead Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element))));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 14, Random Value In Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Global Variable(roundRespawns), perk, 5, 0);
        Set Player Variable At Index(Global Variable(roundRespawns), _extendedPlayerCollection, 15, Empty Array);
        Resurrect(Global Variable(roundRespawns));
        Clear Status(Global Variable(roundRespawns), Burning);
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 14), !=, Null));
            Teleport(Global Variable(roundRespawns), Position Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 14)));
            Set Player Variable(Global Variable(roundRespawns), doorSkipCheck, True);
        End;
        Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Global Variable(roundRespawns), Hero(Ashe));
        Resurrect(Filtered Array(All Dead Players(Team 1), Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)));
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 4, 1);
            Set Global Variable At Index(Died, Global Variable(forindex), Vector(500, Empty Array, 500));
        End;
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), _extendedPlayerCollection, 51, False);
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), _extendedPlayerCollection, 34, False);
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), _extendedPlayerCollection, 50, False);
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), _extendedPlayerCollection, 49, False);
        Set Status(All Living Players(Team 1), Null, Phased Out, 2);
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), monkeyBombsLeft, 3);
        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 37, Add, 1);
        If(Compare(Global Variable(roundnumber), <, 12));
            Modify Global Variable(romanNcounter, Add, 1);
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 38, Random Integer(1, 5));
        Modify Global Variable(costmultiplier, Multiply, Random Real(1, 1));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 115, Random Real(1.01, 1.03));
    }
}

rule("497 start control")
{
    event

        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 30, Condition Count: 4
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(start) == False;
        Global Variable(textListsLoaded) == True;
        Global Variable(settingsViewers) == Null;
        Or(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Jump)), Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, arcadeCodeStep), >=, 6)) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 192, Action Count: 36
    // Largest Action Index: 4 using 17 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 29, Random Integer(0, 5));
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
        If(Compare(Global Variable(Difficulty), >=, 4));
            Set Global Variable(buyheros, Randomized Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 55)));
            Set Global Variable(buyperks, Append To Array(Randomized Array(Array Slice(Global Variable(buyperksHome), 0, 5)), Array Slice(Global Variable(buyperksHome), 5, Count Of(Global Variable(buyperksHome)))));
        Else;
            Set Global Variable(buyheros, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 55));
            Set Global Variable(buyperks, Global Variable(buyperksHome));
        End;
        If(Compare(Workshop Setting Toggle(Custom String("Cheats"), Custom String("Power on from the start"), False, 2), ==, True));
            Set Global Variable(poweron, 1);
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 52, Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, arcadeCodeStep), >=, 6));
        If(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 52));
            Set Global Variable(Difficulty, 1);
            Set Global Variable(difficultyStats, Array Slice(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 30), 0, 6));
        End;
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), arcadeCodeStep, 0);
        Set Global Variable(roundnumber, Subtract(Workshop Setting Integer(Custom String("Cheats"), Custom String("Starting wave"), 1, 1, 100, 1), 1));
        Set Global Variable(start, 1);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 126, Random Integer(1, 100));
        Set Global Variable(startlines, Random Integer(0, 29));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroswap, 0, Hero Of(Event Player));
        Wait(1, Ignore Condition);
        Enable Built-In Game Mode Music;
        Set Global Variable(rngbox, Random Value In Array(Global Variable(mysterylocations)));
        Start Camera(All Players(All Teams), Vector(283.872, 1.875, 118.717), Vector(282.782, 1.329, 109.203), 0);
        Create In-World Text(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("Good Luck..."), Vector(282.782, 1.329, 109.203), 5, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(White), Default Visibility);
        Wait(4, Ignore Condition);
        Stop Camera(All Players(All Teams));
        Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
        Enable Hero HUD(All Players(All Teams));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 9, Workshop Setting Integer(Custom String("Cheats"), Custom String("Starting points"), 500, 500, 1000000, 0));
        Set Player Variable(All Players(All Teams), points, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 9));
        Set Player Variable(All Players(All Teams), points2, Player Variable(All Players(All Teams), points));
        Set Player Variable(All Players(All Teams), cashback, 1);
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 271
rule("105 specific hero ammo and double mag")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 22, Condition Count: 1
    conditions
    {
        Or(Or(Or(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Ability 1)), Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Ability 2))), Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire))), Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire))) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 248, Action Count: 42
    // Largest Action Index: 7 using 24 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire)));
            Skip(Value In Array(Array(3, 0), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(Hero(Moira)), Hero Of(Event Player)), 1)));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, Multiply(0.05, Value In Array(Array(1, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, perk), 4))));
            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
            Loop If Condition Is True;
        End;
        If(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)));
            Skip(Value In Array(Array(19, 0, 6, 12, 14), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(Hero(Doomfist), Hero(D.Va), Hero(Hanzo), Hero(Sigma)), Hero Of(Event Player)), 1)));
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, perk), 4), ==, False));
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, 0.25);
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                Loop If Condition Is True;
            End;
            Skip(13);
            If(Not(Is In Alternate Form(Event Player)));
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, Multiply(0.05, Value In Array(Array(1, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, perk), 4))));
                Wait(0.15, Ignore Condition);
                Loop If Condition Is True;
            End;
            Skip(7);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, Multiply(0.25, Value In Array(Array(1, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, perk), 4))));
            Skip(5);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, Multiply(0.05, Value In Array(Array(1, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, perk), 4))));
            Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, Multiply(0.05, Value In Array(Array(1, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, perk), 4))));
            Wait(1.5, Ignore Condition);
            Loop If Condition Is True;
        Else;
            Skip(Value In Array(Array(12, 0, 4, 9), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(Hero(Reaper), Hero(Cassidy), Hero(Reinhardt)), Hero Of(Event Player)), 1)));
            If(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player));
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, 0.5);
            End;
            Skip(8);
            If(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player));
                Skip If(Compare(Ammo(Event Player, 0), ==, 6), 1);
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, 0.5);
            End;
            Skip(3);
            If(Is Using Ability 2(Event Player));
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, Multiply(0.25, Value In Array(Array(1, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, perk), 4))));
            End;
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 253
rule("172 FOLLOWERS  winston Omnic bomb")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 35, Condition Count: 6
    conditions
    {
        Is Communicating(Event Player, Ultimate Status) == True;
        Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombOwned) == True;
        Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombsLeft) > 0;
        Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombActive) != True;
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
    }

    // Element Count: 217, Action Count: 49
    // Largest Action Index: 29 using 33 Elements
    actions
    {
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombsLeft, Subtract, 1);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombActive, True);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombSlot, Add(Slot Of(Event Player), 4));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 21, Entity Exists(Players In Slot(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombSlot), Team 1)));
        Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 1, Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombSlot));
        Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
        Create Dummy Bot(Hero(Winston), Team 1, Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombSlot), Eye Position(Event Player), Subtract(Left, Left));
        Wait(0.03, Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb, Players In Slot(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombSlot), Team 1));
        Set Player Variable(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb), isMonkeyBomb, True);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, 1);
        Attach Players(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb), Event Player, Vector(Empty Array, Empty Array, 2));
        Start Scaling Player(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb), 0.2, True);
        Start Modifying Hero Voice Lines(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb), 1.3, True);
        Wait(1, Ignore Condition);
        Detach Players(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb));
        Wait(0.2, Ignore Condition);
        Press Button(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb), Button(Jump));
        Apply Impulse(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb), Vector Towards(Event Player, Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb)), 25, To World, Cancel Contrary Motion);
        Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Aura, Color(Orange), Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb), 0.5, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 23, Last Created Entity);
        Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
        Communicate(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb), Countdown);
        Wait(4, Ignore Condition);
        If(Entity Exists(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb)));
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Doomfist Meteor Strike Impact Effect, Color(White), Position Of(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb)), 1);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombTargets, Players Within Radius(Position Of(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb)), 4, Team 2, Surfaces And All Barriers));
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 22, 0);
            While(Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 22), <, Count Of(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombTargets))));
                Damage(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombTargets), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 22)), Null, If-Then-Else(Compare(Distance Between(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombTargets), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 22)), Position Of(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb))), <=, 2), 150, 50));
                Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
                Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 22, Add, 1);
            End;
            Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
            Communicate(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb), Goodbye);
        End;
        Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 23));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 23, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, False);
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
        If(Compare(Players In Slot(Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombSlot), Team 1), ==, Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb)));
            Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 1, Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombSlot));
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb, Null);
        If(And(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 21), Is Alive(Event Player)));
            Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
            Call Subroutine(Subfollowerbuy);
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombActive, False);
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 90
rule("352 FOLLOWERS attack nearest zombie")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 28, Condition Count: 5
    conditions
    {
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == True;
        Slot Of(Event Player) >= 4;
        Slot Of(Event Player) <= 7;
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Player Variable(Event Player, isMonkeyBomb) != True;
    }

    // Element Count: 61, Action Count: 16
    // Largest Action Index: 2 using 26 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, followerFiring, False);
        While(True);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, followerTarget, First Of(Sorted Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 2), And(And(Has Spawned(Current Array Element), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 15)), Is In Line Of Sight(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position(Current Array Element), Barriers Do Not Block LOS))), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element))));
            If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, followerTarget), !=, Null));
                If(Not(Player Variable(Event Player, followerFiring)));
                    Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position(Player Variable(Event Player, followerTarget))), 720, To World, Direction And Turn Rate);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, followerFiring, True);
                End;
                Start Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire));
            Else If(Player Variable(Event Player, followerFiring));
                Stop Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire));
                Stop Facing(Event Player);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, followerFiring, False);
            End;
            Wait(0.25, Abort When False);
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 554
rule("Pack a punch Ongoing - player")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 24, Condition Count: 2
    conditions
    {
        Player Variable(Event Player, Packapunch) >= 1;
        Or(Or(Or(Or(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player), Is Using Ability 2(Event Player)), Is Using Ultimate(Event Player)), Is Firing Primary(Event Player)), Is Firing Secondary(Event Player)) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 529, Action Count: 155
    // Largest Action Index: 111 using 13 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Baptiste)));
            If(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player));
                Remove All Health Pools From Player(Event Player);
                Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
                Add Health Pool To Player(Event Player, Health, 100, False, False);
            End;
        Else If(Is Firing Primary(Event Player));
            If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Bastion)));
                If(Is In Alternate Form(Event Player));
                    Wait(0.5, Abort When False);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDamage, 500);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDelay, 0.2);
                    Call Subroutine(PapRocket);
                    Wait(1.5, Abort When False);
                    Loop If Condition Is True;
                End;
            End;
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Torbjörn)));
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 32), ==, False));
                If(Is Communicating(Event Player, Need Healing));
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, dispencereffect, Empty Array);
                    Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
                    Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Sphere, Color(Green), Position Of(Event Player), 0.8, Visible To);
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, dispencereffect, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                    Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
                    Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Sphere, Color(Green), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 1.3, Empty Array), Position Of(Event Player)), 0.8, Visible To);
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, dispencereffect, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                    Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
                    Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Ring, Color(Green), Position Of(Event Player), 3.5, Visible To);
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, dispencereffect, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 31, Evaluate Once(Position Of(Event Player)));
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 32, 1);
                    Wait(30, Ignore Condition);
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 32, 2);
                    Play Effect(Event Player, Debuff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 60);
                    Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
                    Destroy Effect(First Of(Player Variable(Event Player, dispencereffect)));
                    Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, dispencereffect), 1));
                    Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, dispencereffect), 2));
                    Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
                    Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, dispencereffect, 0, Remove From Array By Index, 0);
                    Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, dispencereffect, 1, Remove From Array By Index, 0);
                    Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, dispencereffect, 2, Remove From Array By Index, 0);
                    Wait(60, Ignore Condition);
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 32, False);
                End;
            End;
        Else If(Is Firing Secondary(Event Player));
            If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Roadhog)));
                If(Compare(Ability Resource(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire)), >, 0));
                    Start Heal Over Time(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 5, Team 1, Surfaces And Enemy Barriers), Event Player, 2, 20);
                    Create Effect(All Players(Team 1), Ring, Color(Orange), Event Player, 5, Visible To Position and Radius);
                    Wait Until(False, 5);
                    Destroy Effect(Last Created Entity);
                End;
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Soldier: 76)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDamage, 500);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDelay, 0.2);
                Call Subroutine(PapRocket);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Echo)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDamage, 500);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDelay, 0.2);
                Call Subroutine(PapStickyBomb);
                Wait(0.2, Abort When False);
                Loop If Condition Is True;
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Juno)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDamage, 500);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDelay, 0);
                Call Subroutine(PapStickyBomb);
                Wait(0.15, Abort When False);
                Loop If Condition Is True;
            End;
        Else If(Is Using Ultimate(Event Player));
            If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Winston)));
                Set Status(Event Player, Event Player, Unkillable, 10);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Symmetra)));
                Set Status(Event Player, Null, Phased Out, 10);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Reinhardt)));
                Set Status(All Living Players(Team 1), Event Player, Knocked Down, 8);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Brigitte)));
                Start Scaling Player(Event Player, 1.5, True);
                Add Health Pool To Player(Event Player, Health, 1000, False, False);
                Wait(8, Ignore Condition);
                Stop Scaling Player(Event Player);
                Remove All Health Pools From Player(Event Player);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Zenyatta)));
                Damage(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 10.5, Team 2, Surfaces And Enemy Barriers), Event Player, 300);
                Wait(1, Abort When False);
                Loop If Condition Is True;
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Zarya)));
                If(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player));
                    Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 1), 0);
                Else If(Is Using Ability 2(Event Player));
                    Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 2), 0);
                End;
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Widowmaker)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, 1);
                If(Compare(Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))), <, 2));
                    Wait Until(False, 4);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, False);
                Else If(Compare(Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))), >=, 2));
                    Wait Until(False, 16);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, False);
                End;
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Lúcio)));
                Set Status(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 3, Team 2, Surfaces And Enemy Barriers), Event Player, Rooted, 3);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Ramattra)));
                Modify Player Variable(All Living Players(Team 1), points, Add, 1000);
                Modify Player Variable(All Living Players(Team 1), magazines, Add, 5);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Genji)));
                If(Is Firing Primary(Event Player));
                    Create Projectile(Reinhardt Fire Strike, Event Player, Eye Position(Event Player), Facing Direction Of(Event Player), To World, Damage, Team 2, 300, 1, 0, Bad Explosion, Explosion Sound, 0, 10, 5, 0, 0, 0);
                End;
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Orisa)));
                Start Scaling Player(Event Player, 2, True);
                Set Damage Dealt(Event Player, 1000);
                Wait Until(False, 8);
                Start Scaling Player(Event Player, 1, True);
                Set Damage Dealt(Event Player, Player Variable(Event Player, damagevariable));
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Kiriko)));
                Set Move Speed(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 15, Team 2, Off), 50);
                Wait(8, Ignore Condition);
                If(Compare(Global Variable(roundnumber), <=, 6));
                    Set Move Speed(All Players(Team 2), Add(52, Multiply(8, Global Variable(roundnumber))));
                Else If(Compare(Global Variable(roundnumber), >, 6));
                    Set Move Speed(All Players(Team 2), 100);
                End;
            End;
        Else If(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player));
            If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Sojourn)));
                Set Status(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 3, Team 2, Off), Event Player, Knocked Down, 4);
            End;
        Else If(Is Using Ability 2(Event Player));
            If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Tracer)));
                Set Status(Players Within Radius(Evaluate Once(Position Of(Event Player)), 5, Team 2, Off), Event Player, Stunned, 4);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Sombra)));
                Wait(1, Ignore Condition);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, 1);
                Wait(4, Ignore Condition);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, False);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(D.Va)));
                Wait(0.2, Abort When False);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDamage, 400);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDelay, 0);
                Call Subroutine(PapRocket);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Lifeweaver)));
                Add Health Pool To Player(Players In View Angle(Event Player, Team 1, 5), Health, 100, False, False);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Pharah)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDamage, 500);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDelay, 0.2);
                Call Subroutine(PapRocket);
                Wait(0.2, Abort When False);
                Loop If Condition Is True;
            End;
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 167
rule("Pack a Punch - Damage dealt")
{
    event
    {
        Player Dealt Damage;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 46, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Array Contains(Array(Hero(Soldier: 76), Hero(Cassidy), Hero(Sigma), Hero(Junkrat), Hero(Moira), Hero(Brigitte), Hero(Doomfist)), Hero Of(Event Player)) == True;
        Player Variable(Event Player, Packapunch) >= 1;
        Or(Or(Or(Or(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player), Is Using Ability 2(Event Player)), Is Using Ultimate(Event Player)), Is Firing Primary(Event Player)), Is Firing Secondary(Event Player)) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 120, Action Count: 29
    // Largest Action Index: 2 using 8 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Cassidy)));
            If(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)));
                Damage(Players Within Radius(Victim, 3, Team 2, Off), Null, 420);
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Good Explosion, Color(White), Victim, 3);
                Wait(0.45, Ignore Condition);
            End;
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Sigma)));
            Damage(Players Within Radius(Victim, 5, Team 2, Off), Null, 200);
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Good Explosion, Color(Blue), Victim, 5);
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Junkrat)));
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Good Explosion, Color(Orange), Victim, 8);
            Damage(Players Within Radius(Victim, 8, Team 2, Off), Null, 300);
        Else If(Is Using Ultimate(Event Player));
            If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Moira)));
                Set Status(Victim, Event Player, Rooted, 4);
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Moira)));
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Good Explosion, Color(Blue), Victim, 5);
                Damage(Players Within Radius(Victim, 5, Team 2, Off), Attacker, 400);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Brigitte)));
                If(Has Status(Victim, Stunned));
                    Set Status(Victim, Event Player, Knocked Down, 4);
                End;
            Else If(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player));
                If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Doomfist)));
                    Set Status(Victim, Event Player, Knocked Down, 4);
                End;
            End;
        End;
    }
}

    }

    // Element Count: 35, Action Count: 4

        Set Player Variable(Event Player, Packapunch, Player Variable(Event Player, papAppliedTier));
    }

    {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, killKind, If-Then-Else(Is Meleeing(Event Player), 2, If-Then-Else(Event Was Critical Hit, 1, 0)));
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, killPoints, Multiply(Add(Value In Array(Array(50, 100, 60), Player Variable(Event Player, killKind)), Multiply(Player Variable(Event Player, heropoints), Player Variable(Event Player, H))), Raise To Power(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 115), Subtract(Global Variable(roundnumber), 1))));
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Add, Player Variable(Event Player, killPoints));
        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 47, Add, Player Variable(Event Player, killPoints));
        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, points)), Player Variable(Event Player, H)), Value In Array(Array(1000, 1000, 250), Player Variable(Event Player, killKind)), Destination And Rate);
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, H), !=, 1));
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 118, Add, Multiply(Value In Array(Array(100, 200, 120), Player Variable(Event Player, killKind)), Player Variable(Event Player, H)));
        End;
        Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, xp, Player Variable(Event Player, currentheroxp), Add, Value In Array(Array(5, 10, 6), Player Variable(Event Player, killKind)));
        Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, Add(Ultimate Charge Percent(Event Player), Divide(Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(difficultyStats), 5), If-Then-Else(Array Contains(All Support Heroes, Hero Of(Event Player)), 1.5, If-Then-Else(Array Contains(All Tank Heroes, Hero Of(Event Player)), 1.25, 1))), 2)));
    }
}

            Set Player Variable(Event Player, heropoints, Multiply(15, Add(Player Variable(Event Player, killKind), 1)));
        Else If(Array Contains(All Support Heroes, Hero Of(Event Player)));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, heropoints, Multiply(5, Add(Player Variable(Event Player, killKind), 1)));

            Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, points)), Player Variable(Event Player, H)), 250, Destination And Rate);
        End;
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, H), !=, 1));
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 118, Add, Multiply(Value In Array(Array(80, 160, 70), Player Variable(Event Player, killKind)), Player Variable(Event Player, H)));
        End;
        Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, xp, Player Variable(Event Player, currentheroxp), Add, Value In Array(Array(5, 10, 7), Player Variable(Event Player, killKind)));
        Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, Add(Ultimate Charge Percent(Event Player), Divide(Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(difficultyStats), 5), If-Then-Else(Array Contains(All Support Heroes, Hero Of(Event Player)), 1.5, If-Then-Else(Array Contains(All Tank Heroes, Hero Of(Event Player)), 1.25, 1))), 2)));

            Set Player Variable(Event Player, cameratoggle, 2);
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 142
rule("26 Revive Player system")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 23, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
        Is True For Any(Global Variable(Died), Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Event Player), <, 4)) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 118, Action Count: 19
    // Largest Action Index: 1 using 14 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, reviveTarget, Players In Slot(Index Of Array Value(Global Variable(Died), First Of(Sorted Array(Global Variable(Died), Distance Between(Current Array Element, Event Player)))), Team 1));
        Chase Player Variable Over Time(Player Variable(Event Player, reviveTarget), sea, 4, Add(2, Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 23), 2)), Destination And Duration);
        Wait(Add(2, Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 23), 2)), Abort When False);
        Resurrect(Player Variable(Event Player, reviveTarget));
        Teleport(Player Variable(Event Player, reviveTarget), Event Player);
        Set Player Variable(Player Variable(Event Player, reviveTarget), doorSkipCheck, True);
        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 23, Add, 1);
        Set Global Variable At Index(Died, Slot Of(Player Variable(Event Player, reviveTarget)), Vector(500, Empty Array, 500));
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Add, Multiply(100, Player Variable(Event Player, H)));
        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, points)), Player Variable(Event Player, H)), 1000, Destination And Rate);
        Skip If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, H), !=, 1), 1);
        Skip If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, H), ==, 1), 1);
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), _extendedPlayerCollection, 51, False);
        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 118, Add, Multiply(100, Player Variable(Event Player, H)));
        Set Status(Player Variable(Event Player, reviveTarget), Null, Phased Out, 5);
        Set Max Health(Player Variable(Event Player, reviveTarget), 100);
        Stop Chasing Player Variable(Player Variable(Event Player, reviveTarget), sea);
        Set Player Variable(Player Variable(Event Player, reviveTarget), sea, 0);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, reviveTarget, Null);
    }
}

        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Crouch)) == True;
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 140, Action Count: 24
    // Largest Action Index: 20 using 14 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Moira Fade Disappear Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 200);
        Remove All Health Pools From Player(Event Player);
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, currentswap), ==, False));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, swapvar, Array(1, 0, 0, 1, 2));
            Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
            Call Subroutine(Subheroswap);
        Else If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, currentswap), ==, 1));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, swapvar, Array(0, 1, 3, 4, 5));
            Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
            Call Subroutine(Subheroswap);
        End;
        Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, heroswap), Player Variable(Event Player, currentswap)));
        Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Health(Event Player, Value In Array(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 40), First Of(Player Variable(Event Player, swap))));
        Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 1), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, abilitycooldown), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, swap), 2)));
        Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 2), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, abilitycooldown), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, swap), 3)));
        Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, abilitycooldown), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, swap), 4)));
        Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 44));
        Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
        Attach Players(Players In Slot(Add(Slot Of(Event Player), 4), Team 1), Event Player, Vector(-0.5, 1.8, -0.2));
        Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Moira Fade Disappear Effect, Color(White), Event Player, 1);
        Wait(1, Ignore Condition);
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 50
rule("save ramattra coins")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        SaveRamattraCoins;
    }

    // Element Count: 49, Action Count: 10
    // Largest Action Index: 0 using 9 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, coinNameIndex, Index Of Array Value(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 12), Custom String("{0}", Event Player)));
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, coinNameIndex), <, 0));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, coinNameIndex, Count Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 12)));
            Set Global Variable(_arrayConstructor, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 12));
            Set Global Variable At Index(_arrayConstructor, Player Variable(Event Player, coinNameIndex), Custom String("{0}", Event Player));
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 12, Global Variable(_arrayConstructor));
        End;
        Set Global Variable(_arrayConstructor, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 13));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_arrayConstructor, Player Variable(Event Player, coinNameIndex), Player Variable(Event Player, ramattraCoins));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 13, Global Variable(_arrayConstructor));
    }
}

    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        SpendRamattraCoins;
    }

    // Element Count: 17, Action Count: 5

            Call Subroutine(SaveRamattraCoins);
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 176
rule("202 Purchase Follower action")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 26, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombActive) != True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Reload)) == True;
        Is True For Any(Global Variable(buyheros), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 149, Action Count: 25
    // Largest Action Index: 4 using 29 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait(1, Abort When False);
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        For Global Variable(i, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(hero)), 1);
            If(And(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global Variable(buyheros), Global Variable(i))), <, 2), Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Global Variable(i)), !=, Null)));
                If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, points), >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(herobuycosts), Global Variable(i)), 3), Raise To Power(0.5, Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses))), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), Player Variable(Event Player, creditcardlimit)), To Nearest)));
                    Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 45, Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Global Variable(i)));
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(herobuycosts), Global Variable(i)), 3), Raise To Power(0.5, Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses))), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), To Nearest));
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points2, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(herobuycosts), Global Variable(i)), 3), Raise To Power(0.5, Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses))), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), To Nearest));
                    Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, 1);
                    Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins);
                    Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
                    Call Subroutine(Subfollowerbuy);
                    If(Not(Player Variable(Event Player, firstFollowerShown)));
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, firstFollowerShown, True);
                        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                        Communicate(Event Player, Emote Up);
                        Call Subroutine(PurchaseFreezeAfterEmote);
                    End;
                Else;
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot, Global Variable(i));
                    Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} {1}", Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot))), Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 65), Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot))));
                End;
                Break;
            End;
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 183
rule("229 buy perks and power activate")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 20, Condition Count: 2
    conditions
    {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Is True For Any(Global Variable(buyperks), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 162, Action Count: 32
    // Largest Action Index: 4 using 26 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        For Global Variable(i, 0, 6, 1);
            If(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global Variable(buyperks), Global Variable(i))), <, 2));
                If(Compare(Global Variable(poweron), ==, 1));
                    If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, points), >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(perkcosts), Global Variable(i)), Raise To Power(0.5, Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses))), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), Player Variable(Event Player, creditcardlimit)), To Nearest)));
                        If(Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, perk), Global Variable(i)), <, 3));
                            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(perkcosts), Global Variable(i)), Raise To Power(0.5, Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses))), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), Player Variable(Event Player, cashback)), To Nearest));
                            Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, perk, Global Variable(i), Add, 1);
                            Set Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot, Global Variable(i));
                            Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(i), ==, 5), 5, 3));
                            Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins);
                            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} {1}", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)), Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 121), Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot))));
                            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                            Play Effect(All Players(Team 1), Ring Explosion Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70);
                            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                            Communicate(Event Player, Emote Up);
                            Call Subroutine(PurchaseFreezeAfterEmote);
                            Break;
                        Else;
                            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} You already have the max tier!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
                            Break;
                        End;
                    Else If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, points), <, Value In Array(Global Variable(perkcosts), Global Variable(i))));
                        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} No points? NO perks!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
                        Break;
                    End;
                Else;
                    Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} The power is not ON!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
                    Break;
                End;
            End;
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 373
rule("202 Purchase hero actions")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 20, Condition Count: 2
    conditions
    {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Is True For Any(Global Variable(buyheros), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 352, Action Count: 48
    // Largest Action Index: 5 using 40 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        For Global Variable(idx, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(buyheros)), 1);
            If(And(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global Variable(buyheros), Global Variable(idx))), <, 2), Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Global Variable(idx)), !=, Null)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot, Global Variable(idx));
                If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot))));
                    If(And(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, points), >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(herobuycosts), Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot)), 2), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), Player Variable(Event Player, creditcardlimit)), To Nearest)), Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines), <, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(Difficulty), ==, 5), 6, 10))));
                        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(herobuycosts), Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot)), 2), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), Player Variable(Event Player, cashback)), To Nearest));
                        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, points)), Player Variable(Event Player, H)), Value In Array(Global Variable(herobuycosts), Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot)), Destination And Rate);
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(Difficulty), ==, 5), 6, 10));
                        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} Magazines refilled!", Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot)))));
                        Break;
                    Else;
                        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines), >=, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(Difficulty), ==, 5), 6, 10)));
                            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You already have full magazines!"));
                        Else If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, points), <, Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(herobuycosts), Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot)), 2)));
                            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} No points? NO magazines!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
                        End;
                    End;
                Else;
                    If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, points), >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(herobuycosts), Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot)), Raise To Power(0.5, Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses))), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), Player Variable(Event Player, creditcardlimit)), To Nearest)));
                        Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot)));
                        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, perk, 5, 0);
                        Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
                        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroswap, Player Variable(Event Player, currentswap), Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot)));
                        Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
                        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroiconswap, Player Variable(Event Player, currentswap), Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot))));
                        If(Compare(Server Load, <, 235));
                            Play Effect(All Players(Team 1), Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70);
                            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Moira Fade Disappear Effect, Color(White), Event Player, 1);
                        End;
                        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(herobuycosts), Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot)), Raise To Power(0.5, Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses))), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), Player Variable(Event Player, cashback)), To Nearest));
                        Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, 1);
                        Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins);
                        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, points)), Player Variable(Event Player, H)), Value In Array(Global Variable(herobuycosts), Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot)), Destination And Rate);
                        Attach Players(Players In Slot(Add(Slot Of(Event Player), 4), Team 1), Event Player, Vector(-0.5, 1.8, -0.2));
                        If(Not(Array Contains(Player Variable(Event Player, allowedheroes), Hero Of(Event Player))));
                            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, allowedheroes, Append To Array, Hero Of(Event Player));
                        End;
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(Difficulty), ==, 5), 6, 10));
                    Else;
                        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} {1}", Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot))), Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 64), Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot))));
                    End;
                    Break;
                End;
            End;
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 91
rule("buy the ending")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 25, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Distance Between(Event Player, Vector(-101.303, -150.36, -17.831)) < 2;
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 65, Action Count: 15
    // Largest Action Index: 2 using 10 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, points), >=, 50000));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Multiply(50000, Player Variable(Event Player, cashback)));
            Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, points)), Player Variable(Event Player, H)), 50000, Destination And Rate);
            Set Player Variable At Index(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element))), _extendedPlayerCollection, 25, True);
            Set Global Variable(slowmoCutscene, 40);
            Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
            Set Global Variable(slowmoCutscene, 0);
            Small Message(All Players(Team 1), Custom String("{0} You really thought this was going to end?", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
            Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
            Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), singlelives, 3);
            Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), perk, 2, 0);
            Kill(All Players(All Teams), Null);
        Else;
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} HA, good luck escaping...", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 248
rule("53  mystery box")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 48, Condition Count: 2
    conditions
    {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        And(And(Compare(Global Variable(start), ==, 1), Compare(Global Variable(rngbox), !=, Null)), Or(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Global Variable(rngbox)), <=, 2), And(Compare(Global Variable(fireSale), ==, True), Is True For Any(Global Variable(fireSaleBoxes), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <=, 2))))) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 199, Action Count: 32
    // Largest Action Index: 0 using 26 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, points), >, Subtract(Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(fireSale), ==, True), 10, 950), Raise To Power(0.5, Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses))), Player Variable(Event Player, creditcardlimit))));
            If(Compare(Server Load, <, 235));
                Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 1.5);
                Play Effect(All Players(Team 1), Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70);
            End;
            Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 1.5);
            Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, First Of(Random Value In Array(Randomized Array(All Heroes))));
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, perk, 5, 0);
            Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
            Resurrect(Event Player);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Multiply(Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(fireSale), ==, True), 10, 950), Raise To Power(0.5, Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses))), Player Variable(Event Player, cashback)));
            Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, 1);
            Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins);
            Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, points)), Player Variable(Event Player, H)), If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(fireSale), ==, True), 10, 950), Destination And Rate);
            If(Compare(Server Load, <, 235));
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Moira Fade Disappear Effect, Color(White), Event Player, 1);
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Buff Explosion Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70);
            End;
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroswap, Player Variable(Event Player, currentswap), Hero Of(Event Player));
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroiconswap, Player Variable(Event Player, currentswap), Hero Icon String(Hero Of(Event Player)));
            If(Not(Array Contains(Player Variable(Event Player, allowedheroes), Hero Of(Event Player))));
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, allowedheroes, Append To Array, Hero Of(Event Player));
            End;
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(Difficulty), ==, 5), 6, 10));
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 34, Add, 1);
            Wait(1, Ignore Condition);
            Skip If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 34), <, Random Integer(6, 15)), 2);
            Set Global Variable(rngbox, Random Value In Array(Global Variable(mysterylocations)));
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 34, False);
        Else If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, points), <, Subtract(Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(fireSale), ==, True), 10, 950), Raise To Power(0.5, Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses))), Player Variable(Event Player, creditcardlimit))));
            Big Message(Event Player, Custom String("Come back when you've got some money buddy!"));
        End;
    }
}

        Team 1;

    }

    // Element Count: 91, Action Count: 5
    // Largest Action Index: 1 using 25 Elements
    actions
    {

        Set Secondary Fire Enabled(Event Player, And(Or(Compare(Global Variable(experiencesystem), ==, False), Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, XPlevel), Player Variable(Event Player, currentheroxp)), >=, 5)), Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines), >, 0)));
        Set Ability 1 Enabled(Event Player, Or(Compare(Global Variable(experiencesystem), ==, False), Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, XPlevel), Player Variable(Event Player, currentheroxp)), >=, 10)));
        Set Ability 2 Enabled(Event Player, Or(Compare(Global Variable(experiencesystem), ==, False), Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, XPlevel), Player Variable(Event Player, currentheroxp)), >=, 15)));
        Set Ultimate Ability Enabled(Event Player, Or(Compare(Global Variable(experiencesystem), ==, False), Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, XPlevel), Player Variable(Event Player, currentheroxp)), >=, 20)));
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 172
rule("Ramattra coins: say I need healing to use one")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 17, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
        Global Variable(start) == 1;
        Is Communicating(Event Player, Need Healing) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 154, Action Count: 10
    // Largest Action Index: 0 using 97 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, coinPrice, If-Then-Else(Or(Or(Is True For Any(Global Variable(buyheros), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)), Is True For Any(Global Variable(bossbuypositions), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2))), And(And(Compare(Global Variable(start), ==, 1), Compare(Global Variable(rngbox), !=, Null)), Or(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Global Variable(rngbox)), <=, 2), And(Compare(Global Variable(fireSale), ==, True), Is True For Any(Global Variable(fireSaleBoxes), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <=, 2)))))), 1, If-Then-Else(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global Variable(buyperks), 5)), <, 2), 5, If-Then-Else(Is True For Any(Array Slice(Global Variable(buyperks), 0, 5), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)), 3, 0))));
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, coinPrice), ==, 0));
        Else If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses), >=, 3));
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Already using 3 Ramattra coins here (an eighth of the price)"));
        Else If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, ramattraCoins), >=, Multiply(Add(Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses), 1), Player Variable(Event Player, coinPrice))));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses, Add, 1);
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} Ramattra coins used: {1}. Price x {2}. Buy as usual", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)), Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses), Raise To Power(0.5, Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses))));
        Else;
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Not enough Ramattra coins: this costs {0} a use (you have {1})", Player Variable(Event Player, coinPrice), Player Variable(Event Player, ramattraCoins)));
        End;
    }
}
