// Rule Element Count: 79
rule("arcade: the secret code")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 32, Condition Count: 5
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(start) == False;
        Global Variable(textListsLoaded) == True;
        Global Variable(settingsViewers) == Null;
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
        Or(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)), Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire))) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 46, Action Count: 9
    // Largest Action Index: 4 using 16 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 14)), >, 1));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, arcadeCodeStep, 0);
        End;
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 14, Total Time Elapsed);
        If(Compare(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)), ==, Compare(Modulo(Player Variable(Event Player, arcadeCodeStep), 2), ==, 0)));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, arcadeCodeStep, Add, 1);
        Else;
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, arcadeCodeStep, If-Then-Else(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)), 1, 0));
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 113
rule("arcade: Soldier, aimbot and overhead camera")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 26, Condition Count: 4
    conditions
    {
        Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 52) == True;
        Global Variable(start) == 1;
        Player Variable(Event Player, outofpsawn) == 1;
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
    }

    // Element Count: 86, Action Count: 16
    // Largest Action Index: 7 using 26 Elements
    actions
    {
        Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, Hero(Soldier: 76));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroswap, 0, Hero(Soldier: 76));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroiconswap, 0, Hero Icon String(Hero(Soldier: 76)));
        Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position(First Of(Sorted Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 2), Not(Is In Spawn Room(Current Array Element))), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element))))), 800, To World, Direction And Turn Rate);
        Start Transforming Throttle(Event Player, 1, 1, Vector(-0.109, Empty Array, 0.994));
        While(Compare(Global Variable(start), ==, 1));
            If(Is Alive(Event Player));
                Start Camera(Event Player, Subtract(Ray Cast Hit Position(Eye Position(Event Player), Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Vector(0.778, 14.866, -7.117)), Null, Event Player, False), Vector(0.024, 0.451, -0.216)), Eye Position(Event Player), 20);
                If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), !=, Hero(Soldier: 76)));
                    Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, Add(Ultimate Charge Percent(Event Player), 40));
                End;
            End;
            Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
        End;
        Stop Facing(Event Player);
        Stop Transforming Throttle(Event Player);
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 172
rule("137  get out of spawn bots")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 17, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is In Spawn Room(Event Player) == True;
        Count Of(Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches)) > 0;
    }

    // Element Count: 154, Action Count: 22
    // Largest Action Index: 9 using 34 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Status(Event Player, Null, Rooted, 9999);
        Wait(Add(0.25, Multiply(Slot Of(Event Player), 0.05)), Abort When False);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick, Random Integer(0, Subtract(Count Of(Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches)), 1)));
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick)), >, 0));
            Set Global Variable At Index(globalActiveBreachHealth, Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick), Max(0, Subtract(Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick)), Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 103))));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, index, Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick));
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Player Variable(Event Player, index)), <=, 0));
                Set Global Variable At Index(color, Player Variable(Event Player, index), Color(Black));
            Else;
                Set Global Variable At Index(color, Player Variable(Event Player, index), Custom Color(Multiply(255, Subtract(1, Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Player Variable(Event Player, index)), 100))), Divide(Multiply(255, Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Player Variable(Event Player, index))), 100), 0, 255));
            End;
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick)), <=, 0));
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Explosion, Color(Orange), Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches), Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick)), 3);
            End;
            Wait(1.5, Abort When False);
        Else;
            Start Forcing Player Outlines(Event Player, All Players(Team 1), True, Custom Color(Random Integer(0, 255), Random Integer(0, 255), Random Integer(0, 255), 255), Default);
            Clear Status(Event Player, Rooted);
            Teleport(Event Player, Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches), Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick)));
            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
        End;
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 138
rule("barricade repair by players")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 25, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Reload)) == True;
        Is True For Any(Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches), Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Event Player), <, 2)) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 112, Action Count: 16
    // Largest Action Index: 8 using 34 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 86, 25);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick, Index Of Array Value(Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches), First Of(Sorted Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches), Distance Between(Current Array Element, Event Player)))));
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick)), <, 100));
            Set Global Variable At Index(globalActiveBreachHealth, Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick), Min(100, Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick)), Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 86))));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, index_0, Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick));
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Player Variable(Event Player, index_0)), <=, 0));
                Set Global Variable At Index(color, Player Variable(Event Player, index_0), Color(Black));
            Else;
                Set Global Variable At Index(color, Player Variable(Event Player, index_0), Custom Color(Multiply(255, Subtract(1, Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Player Variable(Event Player, index_0)), 100))), Divide(Multiply(255, Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Player Variable(Event Player, index_0))), 100), 0, 255));
            End;
            Play Effect(Event Player, Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 50);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Add, 10);
            Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, points)), Player Variable(Event Player, H)), 1000, Destination And Rate);
        End;
        Wait(1, Abort When False);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 111
rule("rebuild barricade effects")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        RebuildBreachEffects;
    }

    // Element Count: 110, Action Count: 19
    // Largest Action Index: 10 using 31 Elements
    actions
    {
        For Global Variable(botspawnindex, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 96)), 1);
            Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 96), Global Variable(botspawnindex)));
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 96, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(color, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(botspawnindex, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches)), 1);
            Set Global Variable(index, Global Variable(botspawnindex));
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Global Variable(index)), <=, 0));
                Set Global Variable At Index(color, Global Variable(index), Color(Black));
            Else;
                Set Global Variable At Index(color, Global Variable(index), Custom Color(Multiply(255, Subtract(1, Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Global Variable(index)), 100))), Divide(Multiply(255, Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Global Variable(index))), 100), 0, 255));
            End;
            Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Aura, Value In Array(Global Variable(color), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(botspawnindex))), Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches), Global Variable(botspawnindex)), Vector(0, 1.5, 0)), 1, Visible To And Color);
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 96, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Aura Sound, Color(White), Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches), Global Variable(botspawnindex)), 50, Visible To And Color);
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 96, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 259
rule("get nearest nodes for spawning")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 6, Condition Count: 1
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(start) == 1;
    }

    // Element Count: 252, Action Count: 41
    // Largest Action Index: 12 using 29 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 103, 25);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 104, 8);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 105, 35);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 109, 15);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 91, 8);
        Set Global Variable(teamPlayers, Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), And(Compare(Player Variable(Current Array Element, outofpsawn), ==, 1), Not(Is In Spawn Room(Current Array Element)))));
        If(And(Compare(Count Of(Global Variable(teamPlayers)), >, 0), Compare(Count Of(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes)), >, 0)));
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 106, Vector(0, 0, 0));
            For Global Variable(botspawnindex, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(teamPlayers)), 1);
                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 106, Add, Position Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(teamPlayers), Global Variable(botspawnindex))));
            End;
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 106, Divide, Count Of(Global Variable(teamPlayers)));
            If(Or(Or(Compare(Global Variable(averagePosition), ==, Null), Compare(Count Of(Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches)), ==, 0)), Compare(Distance Between(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 106), Global Variable(averagePosition)), >, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 109))));
                Set Global Variable(averagePosition, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 106));
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 107, Empty Array);
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 108, Empty Array);
                For Global Variable(botspawnindex, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches)), 1);
                    If(Compare(Distance Between(Global Variable(averagePosition), Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches), Global Variable(botspawnindex))), <, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 105)));
                        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 107, Append To Array, Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches), Global Variable(botspawnindex)));
                        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 108, Append To Array, Value In Array(Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth), Global Variable(botspawnindex)));
                    End;
                End;
                Set Global Variable(candidates, Filtered Array(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes), Compare(Distance Between(Global Variable(averagePosition), Current Array Element), <, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 105))));
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                Set Global Variable(candidates, Filtered Array(Global Variable(candidates), And(Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Closest Player To(Current Array Element, Team 1)), >=, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 104)), Not(Array Contains(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 107), Current Array Element)))));
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 92, Min(Count Of(Global Variable(candidates)), Subtract(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 91), Count Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 107)))));
                For Global Variable(botspawnindex, 0, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 92), 1);
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 93, Random Value In Array(Global Variable(candidates)));
                    Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 107, Append To Array, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 93));
                    Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 108, Append To Array, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(roundnumber), >, 5), 0, 100));
                    Modify Global Variable(candidates, Remove From Array By Value, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 93));
                End;
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                Set Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 107));
                Set Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 108));
                Call Subroutine(RebuildBreachEffects);
            End;
        End;
        Wait(5, Abort When False);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 165
rule("518 spawn bots subroutine")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        wavespawn;
    }

    // Element Count: 164, Action Count: 28
    // Largest Action Index: 21 using 18 Elements
    actions
    {
        Call Subroutine(wavevoicecall);
        Wait Until(Compare(Global Variable(zoneLoading), ==, False), 99999);
        If(Compare(Players In Slot(0, Team 2), ==, Null));
            Create Dummy Bot(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 7), Global Variable(g)), Team 2, 0, First Of(Spawn Points(Team 2)), Subtract(Left, Left));
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(omnicspawn, 0, Slot Of(Players In Slot(0, Team 2)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 8, Players In Slot(0, Team 2));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 40, 0);
        Wait(0.5, Abort When False);
        Call Subroutine(setbotamount);
        For Global Variable(waveSlot, 1, Add(Global Variable(botamount), 1), 2);
            Wait Until(Compare(Global Variable(zoneLoading), ==, False), 99999);
            If(Compare(Players In Slot(Global Variable(waveSlot), Team 2), ==, Null));
                Create Dummy Bot(Value In Array(Global Variable(bot1spawn), Global Variable(g)), Team 2, Global Variable(waveSlot), First Of(Spawn Points(Team 2)), Subtract(Left, Left));
                Set Global Variable At Index(omnicspawn, Global Variable(waveSlot), Slot Of(Players In Slot(Global Variable(waveSlot), Team 2)));
            End;
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 40, Global Variable(waveSlot));
            Wait(0.75, Abort When False);
            If(Compare(Add(Global Variable(waveSlot), 1), <=, Global Variable(botamount)));
                Wait Until(Compare(Global Variable(zoneLoading), ==, False), 99999);
                If(Compare(Players In Slot(Add(Global Variable(waveSlot), 1), Team 2), ==, Null));
                    Create Dummy Bot(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 15), Global Variable(g)), Team 2, Add(Global Variable(waveSlot), 1), First Of(Spawn Points(Team 2)), Subtract(Left, Left));
                    Set Global Variable At Index(omnicspawn, Add(Global Variable(waveSlot), 1), Slot Of(Players In Slot(Add(Global Variable(waveSlot), 1), Team 2)));
                End;
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 40, Add(Global Variable(waveSlot), 1));
                Wait(0.75, Abort When False);
            End;
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 204
rule("519 Spawn wave director")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 16, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(roundnumber) >= 1;
        Global Variable(numberOfZombies) >= 4;
        Is Game In Progress == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 187, Action Count: 25
    // Largest Action Index: 18 using 29 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(Compare(Global Variable(spawntype), <, 3));
            Set Global Variable(g, False);
        End;
        Skip(Value In Array(Array(15, 0, 4, 8, 12), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(False, 1, 2, 3), Global Variable(spawntype)), 1)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 7, Array(Null));
        Set Global Variable(bot1spawn, Array(Hero(Brigitte)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 15, Global Variable(bot1spawn));
        Skip(11);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 7, Array(Null));
        Set Global Variable(bot1spawn, Array(Hero(Torbjörn)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 15, Global Variable(bot1spawn));
        Skip(7);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 7, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(roundnumber), <, 5), Null, Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Random Integer(0, 37))));
        Set Global Variable(bot1spawn, Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Random Integer(0, 37)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 15, Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Random Integer(0, 37)));
        Skip(3);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 7, Array(Null, Hero(Doomfist), Hero(Torbjörn), Hero(Reinhardt), Hero(Winston), Hero(Roadhog), Hero(Junker Queen), Hero(Zenyatta), Hero(Mauga), Hero(Sombra), Hero(Mei), Hero(Reaper), Hero(Ramattra)));
        Set Global Variable(bot1spawn, Array(Hero(Brigitte), Hero(Torbjörn), Hero(Torbjörn), Hero(Moira), Hero(Lúcio), Hero(Torbjörn), Hero(Roadhog), Hero(Moira), Hero(Lúcio), Hero(Mercy), Hero(Junker Queen), Hero(Tracer), Hero(Doomfist)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 15, Array(Hero(Brigitte), Hero(Brigitte), Hero(Reinhardt), Hero(Winston), Hero(Genji), Hero(Winston), Hero(Genji), Hero(Brigitte), Hero(Hanzo), Hero(Mauga), Hero(D.Va), Hero(Echo), Hero(Sombra)));
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
        If(Compare(Global Variable(spawntype), ==, 4));
            Call Subroutine(totalrandomspawn);
        Else;
            Call Subroutine(wavespawn);
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 70
rule("Zombie AI settings and server load gate")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 69, Action Count: 15
    // Largest Action Index: 7 using 14 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait(3, Ignore Condition);
        Set Global Variable(zombieLoadLimit, Workshop Setting Integer(Custom String("Pathfinding"), Custom String("Server load limit for zombie AI extras"), 200, 50, 250, 0));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 20, Workshop Setting Toggle(Custom String("Pathfinding"), Custom String("Zombies pathfind when stuck or you are above them"), True, 1));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 21, Workshop Setting Toggle(Custom String("Pathfinding"), Custom String("Zombies run faster when far away"), True, 2));
        Set Global Variable(zombieExtrasAllowed, True);
        While(True);
            If(Global Variable(zombieExtrasAllowed));
                If(Or(Compare(Server Load Average, >, Global Variable(zombieLoadLimit)), Compare(Server Load, >, Add(Global Variable(zombieLoadLimit), 60))));
                    Set Global Variable(zombieExtrasAllowed, False);
                End;
            Else If(And(Compare(Server Load Average, <, Subtract(Global Variable(zombieLoadLimit), 30)), Compare(Server Load, <, Global Variable(zombieLoadLimit))));
                Set Global Variable(zombieExtrasAllowed, True);
            End;
            Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 471
rule("Zombie chase: straight at you, pathfinding only when stuck or you are above")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 23, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is In Spawn Room(Event Player) == False;
        And(Array Contains(Global Variable(bossBots), Event Player), Compare(Event Player, !=, Global Variable(bossBody))) == False;
    }

    // Element Count: 447, Action Count: 58
    // Largest Action Index: 16 using 47 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget, First Of(Sorted Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Compare(Player Variable(Current Array Element, p_T), ==, 0)), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element))));
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget), ==, Null));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget, First Of(Sorted Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element))));
        End;
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 10, If-Then-Else(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget), ==, Null), 9999, Distance Between(Event Player, Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget))));
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget), ==, Null));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination, Null);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieMode, 0);
            Stop Throttle In Direction(Event Player);
        Else;
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieFlatDistance, Distance Between(Multiply(Position Of(Event Player), Vector(1, Empty Array, 1)), Multiply(Position Of(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget)), Vector(1, Empty Array, 1))));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieHeightDifference, Subtract(Y Component Of(Position Of(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget))), Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player))));
            If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieMode), ==, 1));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 9, And(Compare(Absolute Value(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieHeightDifference)), <, 1.2), Or(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieFlatDistance), <, 3), And(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, Player Variable(Event Player, zombieModeSince)), >, 3), Is In Line Of Sight(Add(Position Of(Event Player), Up), Add(Position Of(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget)), Up), Barriers Do Not Block LOS)))));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 8, And(And(And(And(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 20), Global Variable(pfMapReady)), Not(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 9))), Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, Player Variable(Event Player, zombieModeSince)), <, 20)), Compare(Server Load, <, Add(Global Variable(zombieLoadLimit), 60))));
            Else;
                If(And(And(And(And(And(And(And(And(Compare(Horizontal Speed Of(Event Player), <, 1.5), Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieFlatDistance), >, 3)), Not(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieBusy))), Is On Ground(Event Player)), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Frozen))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Stunned))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Knocked Down))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Asleep))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Hacked))));
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, zombieStuckChecks, Add, 1);
                Else;
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieStuckChecks, 0);
                End;
                If(Compare(Absolute Value(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieHeightDifference)), >, 1.8));
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAboveChecks, Add, 1);
                Else;
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAboveChecks, 0);
                End;
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 8, And(And(And(And(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 20), Global Variable(pfMapReady)), Global Variable(zombieExtrasAllowed)), Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, Player Variable(Event Player, zombieModeSince)), >, 1.5)), Or(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieStuckChecks), >=, 3), Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAboveChecks), >=, 2))));
            End;
            If(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 8));
                If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieMode), !=, 1));
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieMode, 1);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieModeSince, Total Time Elapsed);
                    Call Subroutine(pfLookAtDestination);
                End;
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination, Position Of(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget)));
            Else;
                If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieMode), !=, 2));
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieMode, 2);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieModeSince, Total Time Elapsed);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieStuckChecks, 0);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAboveChecks, 0);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination, Null);
                    Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget))), 400, To World, Direction And Turn Rate);
                End;
                If(Not(Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsPathfinding)));
                    Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Direction Towards(Position Of(Event Player), Position Of(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget))), 1, To World, Replace Existing Throttle, Direction And Magnitude);
                End;
            End;
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieSpeedBoost, If-Then-Else(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 21), Add(1, Min(Multiply(Max(Subtract(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieFlatDistance), 15), 0), 0.04), 0.6)), 1));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieSpeedBoost, Divide(Round To Integer(Multiply(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieSpeedBoost), 10), Down), 10));
            If(And(Not(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieBusy)), Compare(Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(roundnumber), <=, 6), Add(80, Multiply(8, Global Variable(roundnumber))), 128), Player Variable(Event Player, zombieSpeedBoost)), !=, Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAppliedSpeed))));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAppliedSpeed, Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(roundnumber), <=, 6), Add(80, Multiply(8, Global Variable(roundnumber))), 128), Player Variable(Event Player, zombieSpeedBoost)));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 4, Divide(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAppliedSpeed), 100));
                Set Move Speed(Event Player, Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAppliedSpeed));
            End;
        End;
        Wait(If-Then-Else(Global Variable(zombieExtrasAllowed), 0.25, 0.5), Abort When False);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 375
rule("【PF Decompress】Decompress map")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 5, Condition Count: 1
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(pfDecompress) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 369, Action Count: 54
    // Largest Action Index: 6 using 121 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Global Variable(pfMapReady, False);
        Set Global Variable(pfcompressionTable, Custom String(" {0}", Global Variable(decodeTable)));
        Set Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_neighbors, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Count Of(First Of(Global Variable(pfDecompress))), 1);
            For Global Variable(pfI2, 0, String Length(Value In Array(First Of(Global Variable(pfDecompress)), Global Variable(pfI1))), 6);
                Set Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes, Append To Array(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes), Vector(Divide(Subtract(Add(Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global Variable(pfcompressionTable), Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global Variable(pfDecompress)), Global Variable(pfI1)), Global Variable(pfI2))), 1024), Index Of String Char(Global Variable(pfcompressionTable), Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global Variable(pfDecompress)), Global Variable(pfI1)), Add(Global Variable(pfI2), 1)))), 524288), 100), Divide(Subtract(Add(Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global Variable(pfcompressionTable), Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global Variable(pfDecompress)), Global Variable(pfI1)), Add(Global Variable(pfI2), 2))), 1024), Index Of String Char(Global Variable(pfcompressionTable), Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global Variable(pfDecompress)), Global Variable(pfI1)), Add(Global Variable(pfI2), 3)))), 524288), 100), Divide(Subtract(Add(Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global Variable(pfcompressionTable), Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global Variable(pfDecompress)), Global Variable(pfI1)), Add(Global Variable(pfI2), 4))), 1024), Index Of String Char(Global Variable(pfcompressionTable), Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global Variable(pfDecompress)), Global Variable(pfI1)), Add(Global Variable(pfI2), 5)))), 524288), 100))));
                If(Not(Modulo(Global Variable(pfI2), 24)));
                    Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                End;
            End;
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 17, 0);
        Set Global Variable(pfcurrentNeighbors, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(pfDecompress), 1)), 1);
            For Global Variable(pfI2, 0, String Length(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(pfDecompress), 1), Global Variable(pfI1))), 1);
                Set Global Variable(pfnext, Index Of String Char(Global Variable(pfcompressionTable), Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(pfDecompress), 1), Global Variable(pfI1)), Global Variable(pfI2))));
                If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 17), ==, 0));
                    If(Not(Global Variable(pfnext)));
                        Set Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_neighbors, Append To Array(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_neighbors), Array(Global Variable(pfcurrentNeighbors))));
                        Set Global Variable(pfcurrentNeighbors, Empty Array);
                    Else;
                        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 17, 1);
                        Set Global Variable(pfcurrentNeighbors, Append To Array(Global Variable(pfcurrentNeighbors), Array(Array(Subtract(Global Variable(pfnext), 1), Empty Array))));
                    End;
                Else If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 17), ==, 1));
                    If(Not(Global Variable(pfnext)));
                        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 17, 0);
                    Else;
                        Set Global Variable At Index(pfcurrentNeighbors, Subtract(Count Of(Global Variable(pfcurrentNeighbors)), 1), Array(First Of(Last Of(Global Variable(pfcurrentNeighbors))), Append To Array(Value In Array(Last Of(Global Variable(pfcurrentNeighbors)), 1), Subtract(Global Variable(pfnext), 1))));
                    End;
                End;
                If(Not(Modulo(Global Variable(pfI2), 20)));
                    Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                End;
            End;
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        End;
        Set Global Variable(pfNodeComponent, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes)), 1);
            Set Global Variable(pfNodeComponent, Append To Array(Global Variable(pfNodeComponent), Index Of String Char(Global Variable(pfcompressionTable), Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(pfDecompress), 3), Round To Integer(Divide(Global Variable(pfI1), 42), Down)), Add(Multiply(Modulo(Global Variable(pfI1), 42), 3), 2)))));
            If(Not(Modulo(Global Variable(pfI1), 20)));
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            End;
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 18, Mapped Array(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes), Current Array Index));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 19, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Add(Last Of(Sorted Array(Global Variable(pfNodeComponent), Current Array Element)), 1), 1);
            Set Global Variable(_arrayConstructor, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 19));
            Set Global Variable At Index(_arrayConstructor, Global Variable(pfI1), Filtered Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 18), Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(pfNodeComponent), Current Array Element), ==, Global Variable(pfI1))));
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 19, Global Variable(_arrayConstructor));
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        End;
        Set Global Variable(pfMapReady, True);
    }
}

    }

// Rule Element Count: 157
rule("【PF Core】Update & Execute")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 25, Condition Count: 2
    conditions
    {
        Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination) == True;
        Or(Not(Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget)), Compare(Distance Between(Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination), Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget)), >, 5)) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 131, Action Count: 15
    // Largest Action Index: 8 using 50 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(Not(Global Variable(pfMapReady)));
            Wait Until(Global Variable(pfMapReady), 99999);
        End;
        Wait(Random Real(0, 0.3), Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget, Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination));
        If(Or(Not(Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsPathfinding)), Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex), ==, -1)));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex, First Of(Sorted Array(Array Slice(Sorted Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 18), Distance Between(Value In Array(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes), Current Array Element), Position Of(Event Player))), 0, 5), Not(Is In Line Of Sight(Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes), Current Array Element), Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), Add(Position Of(Event Player), Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), Barriers Do Not Block LOS)))));
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfGoalNode, First Of(Sorted Array(Array Slice(Sorted Array(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 19), Value In Array(Global Variable(pfNodeComponent), Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex))), Distance Between(Value In Array(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes), Current Array Element), Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget))), 0, 5), Not(Is In Line Of Sight(Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes), Current Array Element), Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), Add(Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget), Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), Barriers Do Not Block LOS)))));
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsPathfinding, True);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition, Value In Array(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes), Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex)));
        Call Subroutine(pfUpdateNodeMeta);
        Call Subroutine(pfWalkToDestination);
        Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

    }

// Rule Element Count: 126
rule("【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Look up next hop (Floyd-Warshall, RLE base 1024)")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        pfLookupNextHop;
    }

    // Element Count: 125, Action Count: 14
    // Largest Action Index: 0 using 61 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfHopPosition, Add(Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global Variable(pfcompressionTable), Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(pfDecompress), 3), Round To Integer(Divide(Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex), 42), Down)), Multiply(Modulo(Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex), 42), 3))), 1024), Index Of String Char(Global Variable(pfcompressionTable), Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(pfDecompress), 3), Round To Integer(Divide(Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex), 42), Down)), Add(Multiply(Modulo(Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex), 42), 3), 1)))));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 5, Player Variable(Event Player, pfGoalNode));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 6, 0);
        Call Subroutine(pfReadNextHopChar);
        While(Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 5), >, Modulo(Player Variable(Event Player, pfHopChar), 128)));
            Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 5, Subtract, Add(Modulo(Player Variable(Event Player, pfHopChar), 128), 1));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, pfHopPosition, Add, 1);
            Call Subroutine(pfReadNextHopChar);
            Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 6, Add, 1);
            If(Compare(Modulo(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 6), 25), ==, 0));
                Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
            End;
        End;
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 7, Round To Integer(Divide(Player Variable(Event Player, pfHopChar), 128), Down));
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 100
rule("【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Progress event player")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        pfProgressEventPlayer;
    }

    // Element Count: 99, Action Count: 17
    // Largest Action Index: 12 using 19 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex), ==, Player Variable(Event Player, pfGoalNode)));
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 2, Empty Array);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex, -1);
        Else;
            Call Subroutine(pfLookupNextHop);
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 7), >=, Count Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_neighbors), Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex)))));
                Log To Inspector(Custom String("Pathfinder {0}: no path from node {1} to node {2}.", Event Player, Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex), Player Variable(Event Player, pfGoalNode)));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 2, Empty Array);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex, -1);
            Else;
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfPreviousNode, Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex, First Of(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_neighbors), Player Variable(Event Player, pfPreviousNode)), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 7))));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 2, Value In Array(First Of(Filtered Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_neighbors), Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex)), Compare(First Of(Current Array Element), ==, Player Variable(Event Player, pfPreviousNode)))), 1));
            End;
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition, If-Then-Else(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex), ==, -1), Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination), Value In Array(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes), Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex))));
        Call Subroutine(pfUpdateNodeMeta);
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 141
rule("【PF Core】Watch progress (checked every 4 ticks)")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 6, Condition Count: 1
    conditions
    {
        Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsPathfinding) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 134, Action Count: 18
    // Largest Action Index: 3 using 60 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsStuck, False);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfSlowSince, 0);
        While(Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsPathfinding));
            If(And(And(And(Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition), Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex), !=, -1)), Compare(Distance Between(Multiply(Position Of(Event Player), Vector(1, Empty Array, 1)), Multiply(Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition), Vector(1, Empty Array, 1))), <=, Multiply(0.6, Max(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 4), 1)))), Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player)), Y Component Of(Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition)))), <=, 1.5)));
                Call Subroutine(pfProgressEventPlayer);
            Else If(And(Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition), Compare(Distance Between(Divide(Add(Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingFromPosition), Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition)), 2), Event Player), >, Multiply(Add(Distance Between(Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingFromPosition), Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition)), 4), 1.2))));
                Call Subroutine(pfRecalibrate);
            End;
            If(Or(Not(Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition)), Compare(Magnitude Of(Velocity Of(Event Player)), >=, 3)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfSlowSince, 0);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsStuck, False);
            Else If(Not(Player Variable(Event Player, pfSlowSince)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfSlowSince, Total Time Elapsed);
            Else If(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, Player Variable(Event Player, pfSlowSince)), >, 2.1));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsStuck, True);
            End;
            Wait(0.064, Ignore Condition);
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 224
rule("【PF Core】Insurance")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 12, Condition Count: 2
    conditions
    {
        Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsPathfinding) == True;
        Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 211, Action Count: 15
    // Largest Action Index: 4 using 80 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(And(And(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex), ==, Player Variable(Event Player, pfGoalNode)), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget)), <, Distance Between(Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition), Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget)))), Is In Line Of Sight(Add(Position Of(Event Player), Vector(Empty Array, 0.25, Empty Array)), Add(Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget), Vector(Empty Array, 0.25, Empty Array)), Barriers Do Not Block LOS)));
            Call Subroutine(pfProgressEventPlayer);
        Else If(And(And(And(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition), !=, Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination)), Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex), ==, -1)), Compare(Distance Between(Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget), Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination)), <, 5)), Is In Line Of Sight(Add(Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget), Up), Add(Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination), Up), Barriers Do Not Block LOS)));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition, Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination));
        Else If(And(And(And(And(And(And(And(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition)), >, 0.4), Not(Or(Not(Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget)), Compare(Distance Between(Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination), Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget)), >, 5)))), Or(Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsStuck), Compare(Subtract(Distance Between(Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingFromPosition), Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition)), Multiply(4.95, Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 1)))), <, 0))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Rooted))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Frozen))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Asleep))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Knocked Down))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Stunned))));
            If(Compare(Subtract(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 3), 2), ==, Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex)));
                Log To Inspector(Custom String("Pathfinder {0} struggled to reach node {1} at {2}. Try adjusting your map in the editor.", Event Player, Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex), Value In Array(Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes), Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex))));
                Teleport(Event Player, Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition));
            Else;
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 3, Add(Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex), 2));
                Call Subroutine(pfRecalibrate);
            End;
        End;
        Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 884
rule("Collision Logic")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 16, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Global Variable(start) == 1;
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
    }

    // Element Count: 867, Action Count: 84
    // Largest Action Index: 42 using 78 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 46), ==, 1));
            Wait Until(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 46), ==, 0), 999999995904);
            Wait(1.5, Ignore Condition);
        End;
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, outofpsawn), ==, 0));
            Wait Until(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, outofpsawn), ==, 1), 999999995904);
            Wait(1.5, Ignore Condition);
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, lastsavedpos, Divide(Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Position Of(Event Player)), 2));
        Wait(Add(Add(0.16, Divide(Multiply(0.32, Server Load Peak), 255)), Multiply(Slot Of(Event Player), 0.016)), Ignore Condition);
        Wait Until(Compare(Max(Server Load Average, Server Load), <, 245), 0.5);
        If(Or(Compare(Distance Between(Player Variable(Event Player, lastsavedpos), Event Player), >, 40), Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, doorSkipCheck), ==, True)));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, doorSkipCheck, False);
            Loop If Condition Is True;
            Abort;
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, closestwall, Filtered Array(Mapped Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Current Array Index), Or(Or(Compare(Distance Between(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Center), Current Array Element), Event Player), <=, Distance Between(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Center), Current Array Element), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Current Array Element))), Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, active_wall), Current Array Element), ==, 1)), Compare(Compare(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Center), Current Array Element), Player Variable(Event Player, lastsavedpos)), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Facing), Current Array Element)), >, 0), !=, Compare(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Center), Current Array Element), Event Player), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Facing), Current Array Element)), >, 0)))));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 11, 0);
        While(Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 11), <, Count Of(Player Variable(Event Player, closestwall))));
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_State), Global Variable(z)), ==, 0));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, active_wall, Global Variable(z), 0);
                Set Move Speed(Event Player, 100);
            End;
            Set Global Variable(z, Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, closestwall), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 11)));
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_State), Global Variable(z)), ==, 1));
                If(And(Compare(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Global Variable(z))), >=, Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player))), Compare(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Global Variable(z))), <=, Y Component Of(Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Vector(Empty Array, 0.2, Empty Array))))));
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, closestbodypos, Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Global Variable(z)));
                Else If(And(Compare(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_OppositeCorner), Global Variable(z))), >=, Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player))), Compare(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_OppositeCorner), Global Variable(z))), <=, Y Component Of(Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Vector(Empty Array, 0.2, Empty Array))))));
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, closestbodypos, Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_OppositeCorner), Global Variable(z)));
                Else;
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, closestbodypos, Position Of(Event Player));
                End;
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, fullbodypos, Vector(X Component Of(Eye Position(Event Player)), Y Component Of(Player Variable(Event Player, closestbodypos)), Z Component Of(Eye Position(Event Player))));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, filterpos, Add(Player Variable(Event Player, fullbodypos), Divide(Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Facing), Global Variable(z)), Dot Product(Subtract(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Center), Global Variable(z)), Player Variable(Event Player, fullbodypos)), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Facing), Global Variable(z)))), Dot Product(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Facing), Global Variable(z)), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Facing), Global Variable(z))))));
                If(Compare(Compare(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Center), Global Variable(z)), Player Variable(Event Player, lastsavedpos)), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Facing), Global Variable(z))), >, 0), !=, Compare(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Center), Global Variable(z)), Player Variable(Event Player, fullbodypos)), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Facing), Global Variable(z))), >, 0)));
                    Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 12, Divide(Dot Product(Subtract(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Center), Global Variable(z)), Player Variable(Event Player, fullbodypos)), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Facing), Global Variable(z))), Dot Product(Direction Towards(Player Variable(Event Player, lastsavedpos), Player Variable(Event Player, fullbodypos)), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Facing), Global Variable(z)))));
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, prevpos_intersection, Add(Player Variable(Event Player, fullbodypos), Multiply(Multiply(Direction Towards(Player Variable(Event Player, lastsavedpos), Player Variable(Event Player, fullbodypos)), Vector(1, 1, 1)), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 12))));
                    If(And(Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Dot Product(Subtract(Player Variable(Event Player, prevpos_intersection), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Global Variable(z))), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Side), Global Variable(z))), Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Width), Global Variable(z)), 2))), <=, Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Width), Global Variable(z)), 2)), Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Y Component Of(Player Variable(Event Player, prevpos_intersection)), Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Center), Global Variable(z))))), <=, Divide(Subtract(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_OppositeCorner), Global Variable(z))), Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Global Variable(z)))), 2))));
                        Cancel Primary Action(Event Player);
                        Teleport(Event Player, Add(Player Variable(Event Player, prevpos_intersection), Multiply(Multiply(Direction Towards(Player Variable(Event Player, prevpos_intersection), Player Variable(Event Player, lastsavedpos)), Vector(1, Empty Array, 1)), 2)));
                    End;
                End;
                If(And(Compare(Distance Between(Player Variable(Event Player, fullbodypos), Player Variable(Event Player, filterpos)), <=, 4), And(Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Dot Product(Subtract(Player Variable(Event Player, filterpos), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Global Variable(z))), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Side), Global Variable(z))), Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Width), Global Variable(z)), 2))), <=, Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Width), Global Variable(z)), 2)), Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Y Component Of(Player Variable(Event Player, filterpos)), Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Center), Global Variable(z))))), <=, Divide(Subtract(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_OppositeCorner), Global Variable(z))), Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Global Variable(z)))), 2)))));
                    If(Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, active_wall), Global Variable(z)), ==, False));
                        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, active_wall, Global Variable(z), 1);
                        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 1), ==, False));
                            Set Gravity(Event Player, 100);
                        End;
                    End;
                    Apply Impulse(Event Player, Multiply(Direction Towards(Player Variable(Event Player, filterpos), Player Variable(Event Player, fullbodypos)), Vector(1, Empty Array, 1)), 1, To World, Cancel Contrary Motion);
                    If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, doorNotif), ==, 0));
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, doorNotif, 1);
                    End;
                    If(And(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)), Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, points), >=, Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(1000, Global Variable(costmultiplier)), Value In Array(Array(1, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, perk), 3))), Player Variable(Event Player, creditcardlimit)))));
                        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Good Explosion, Color(White), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Center), Global Variable(z)), 50);
                        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Lúcio Sound Barrier Cast Sound, Color(White), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Center), Global Variable(z)), 100);
                        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Multiply(1000, Global Variable(costmultiplier)), Value In Array(Array(1, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, perk), 3))), Player Variable(Event Player, cashback)), To Nearest));
                        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 80, Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Zone), Global Variable(z)));
                        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 79, If-Then-Else(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 80), ==, -1), Array(Global Variable(z)), Filtered Array(Mapped Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Current Array Index), Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Zone), Current Array Element), ==, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 80)))));
                        For Global Variable(i, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 79)), 1);
                            Set Global Variable(z, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 79), Global Variable(i)));
                            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_State), Global Variable(z)), ==, 1));
                                Set Global Variable(doorIndex, Global Variable(z));
                                Call Subroutine(DestroyDoorEffects);
                                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_State, Global Variable(z), 0);
                                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, active_wall, Global Variable(z), 0);
                                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 56, Append To Array, Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 3), Global Variable(z)));
                            End;
                        End;
                        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Zone bought!"));
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, doorNotif, 0);
                        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, points)), Player Variable(Event Player, H)), 1000, Destination And Rate);
                        Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
                        Set Move Speed(Event Player, 100);
                    End;
                    Set Move Speed(Event Player, Subtract(100, Multiply(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Add(Eye Position(Event Player), World Vector Of(Throttle Of(Event Player), Event Player, Rotation))), Multiply(Direction Towards(Player Variable(Event Player, filterpos), Player Variable(Event Player, fullbodypos)), -1)), 100)));
                Else;
                    Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, active_wall, Global Variable(z), 0);
                    Set Move Speed(Event Player, 100);
                End;
            End;
            Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 11, Add, 1);
        End;
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 45
rule("Reset door notif")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 7, Condition Count: 1
    conditions
    {
        Player Variable(Event Player, doorNotif) == 1;
    }

    // Element Count: 37, Action Count: 5
    // Largest Action Index: 0 using 30 Elements
    actions
    {
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Hold {0} to buy zone - cost: {1}", Input Binding String(Button(Interact)), Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(1000, Global Variable(costmultiplier)), Value In Array(Array(1, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, perk), 3))), To Nearest)));
        Wait(4, Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, doorNotif, 0);
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 100
rule("make the door effects")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        makedoors;
    }

    // Element Count: 99, Action Count: 15
    // Largest Action Index: 7 using 35 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait(3, Ignore Condition);
        Wait Until(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 2), ==, 1), 99999);
        Set Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer, 0);
        While(Compare(Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer), <, Count Of(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner))));
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_State), Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer)), ==, 1));
                Create Beam Effect(All Players(Team 1), Junkrat Trap Chain Beam, Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer)), Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer)), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_BeamSpan), Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer))), Color(Red), Visible To);
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_BeamA, Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer), Last Created Entity);
                Create Beam Effect(All Players(Team 1), Junkrat Trap Chain Beam, Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer)), Vector(Empty Array, Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_BeamSpan), Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer))), Empty Array)), Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer)), Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_BeamSpan), Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer)), Vector(1, Empty Array, 1))), Color(Red), Visible To);
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_BeamB, Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer), Last Created Entity);
                If(Not(Modulo(Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer), 3)));
                    Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                End;
            End;
            Modify Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer, Add, 1);
        End;
    }
}

    actions

    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 157
rule("anticrash: slow motion")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 77, Condition Count: 1
    conditions
    {
        Min(If-Then-Else(And(Compare(Global Variable(start), ==, 1), Compare(Server Load, >=, 150)), Max(93, Round To Integer(Subtract(100, Divide(Multiply(7, Subtract(Server Load, 150)), 105)), Up)), 100), Min(If-Then-Else(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 42), 50, 100), Min(If-Then-Else(Global Variable(slowmoCutscene), Global Variable(slowmoCutscene), 100), If-Then-Else(And(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 44), Compare(Global Variable(start), ==, 1)), 10, 100)))) != Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 43);
    }

    // Element Count: 79, Action Count: 4
    // Largest Action Index: 0 using 71 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 43, Min(If-Then-Else(And(Compare(Global Variable(start), ==, 1), Compare(Server Load, >=, 150)), Max(93, Round To Integer(Subtract(100, Divide(Multiply(7, Subtract(Server Load, 150)), 105)), Up)), 100), Min(If-Then-Else(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 42), 50, 100), Min(If-Then-Else(Global Variable(slowmoCutscene), Global Variable(slowmoCutscene), 100), If-Then-Else(And(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 44), Compare(Global Variable(start), ==, 1)), 10, 100)))));
        Set Slow Motion(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 43));
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 29
rule("anticrash: emergency slow motion")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 11, Condition Count: 2
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(start) == 1;
        Server Load >= 250;
    }

    // Element Count: 17, Action Count: 6
    // Largest Action Index: 3 using 6 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait(2, Abort When False);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 44, True);
        Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("Server overloaded: slowing down time"));
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 200), 3);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 44, False);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 59
rule("120  Remove bots when players join")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 58, Action Count: 13
    // Largest Action Index: 1 using 8 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(Compare(Number Of Players(Team 1), ==, 2));
            For Global Variable(botRemoveSlot, 14, Compare(Global Variable(botRemoveSlot), <=, 17), 1);
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Global Variable(botRemoveSlot));
            End;
        Else If(Compare(Number Of Players(Team 1), ==, 3));
            For Global Variable(botRemoveSlot, 10, Compare(Global Variable(botRemoveSlot), <=, 17), 1);
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Global Variable(botRemoveSlot));
            End;
        Else If(Compare(Number Of Players(Team 1), ==, 4));
            For Global Variable(botRemoveSlot, 8, Compare(Global Variable(botRemoveSlot), <=, 17), 1);
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Global Variable(botRemoveSlot));
            End;
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 130
rule("Subroutine decompressNSub")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        decompressNSub;
    }

    // Element Count: 129, Action Count: 11
    // Largest Action Index: 4 using 91 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Global Variable(decodeOut, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(decodeString, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(decodeIn), 3)), 1);
            Set Global Variable(decodePosition, 0);
            While(Compare(Global Variable(decodePosition), <, String Length(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(decodeIn), 3), Global Variable(decodeString)))));
                Set Global Variable(decodeOut, Append To Array(Global Variable(decodeOut), Divide(Add(Add(Add(Index Of String Char(Global Variable(decodeTable), Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(decodeIn), 3), Global Variable(decodeString)), Global Variable(decodePosition))), If-Then-Else(Compare(First Of(Global Variable(decodeIn)), >, 1), Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global Variable(decodeTable), Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(decodeIn), 3), Global Variable(decodeString)), Add(Global Variable(decodePosition), 1))), 1024), 0)), If-Then-Else(Compare(First Of(Global Variable(decodeIn)), >, 2), Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global Variable(decodeTable), Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(decodeIn), 3), Global Variable(decodeString)), Add(Global Variable(decodePosition), 2))), 1048576), 0)), Value In Array(Global Variable(decodeIn), 2)), Value In Array(Global Variable(decodeIn), 1))));
                If(Not(Modulo(Count Of(Global Variable(decodeOut)), 20)));
                    Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                End;
                Modify Global Variable(decodePosition, Add, First Of(Global Variable(decodeIn)));
            End;
        End;
    }
}

    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        decompressVSub;
    }

// Rule Element Count: 241
rule("488 button held teleporter")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 23, Condition Count: 2
    conditions
    {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Count Of(Players Within Radius(Value In Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Global Variable(telespot)), 3, Team 1, Off)) > 0;
    }

    // Element Count: 217, Action Count: 43
    // Largest Action Index: 0 using 29 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(Compare(Count Of(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))), ==, Count Of(Filtered Array(Players Within Radius(Value In Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Global Variable(telespot)), 3, Team 1, Off), And(Is Alive(Current Array Element), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))))));
            If(Compare(Global Variable(teleporterwait), ==, False));
                Set Global Variable(teleporterwait, True);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, teleporterEffects, Empty Array);
                Create Effect(Event Player, Ana Nano Boosted Effect, Team Of(Event Player), Event Player, 1, Visible To Position and Radius);
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, teleporterEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                Create Effect(Local Player, Winston Tesla Cannon Target Effect, Color(Black), Event Player, 80, Visible To Position and Radius);
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, teleporterEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                Set Status(Players Within Radius(Value In Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Global Variable(telespot)), 5, Team 2, Off), Event Player, Stunned, 0.25);
                Destroy Effect(First Of(Player Variable(Event Player, teleporterEffects)));
                Play Effect(Local Player, Ring Explosion Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 80);
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                Kill(Players Within Radius(Value In Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Global Variable(telespot)), 5, Team 2, Off), First Of(All Living Players(Team 1)));
                Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, teleporterEffects), 1));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, teleporterEffects, Empty Array);
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 120, Color(Red));
                If(Compare(Count Of(Global Variable(teleporterpositions)), >, 0));
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 136, False);
                    Set Global Variable(telespot, Modulo(Add(Global Variable(telespot), 1), Count Of(Global Variable(teleporterpositions))));
                    Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 46, 1);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, lastsavedpos, Null);
                    Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
                    Wait Until(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 136), ==, True), 15);
                    Teleport(All Players(Team 1), Value In Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Global Variable(telespot)));
                    For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 4, 1);
                        If(And(Is Dead(Players In Slot(Global Variable(forindex), Team 1)), Compare(Distance Between(Value In Array(Global Variable(Died), Global Variable(forindex)), Vector(500, Empty Array, 500)), >, 1)));
                            Set Global Variable At Index(Died, Global Variable(forindex), Nearest Walkable Position(Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Global Variable(telespot)), Vector(Subtract(Multiply(Global Variable(forindex), 1.5), 2.25), Empty Array, 2))));
                        End;
                    End;
                    Skip(Value In Array(Array(5, 0, 2, 4), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(0, 1, 2), Global Variable(telespot)), 1)));
                    Call Subroutine(zone1);
                    Skip(3);
                    Call Subroutine(zone2);
                    Skip(1);
                    Call Subroutine(zone3);
                End;
                Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 46, 0);
                Set Global Variable(doorsNeedDrawing, True);
            End;
        End;
    }
}
