// Rule Element Count: 66
rule("wave boss dispatch")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        SmDispatch;
    }

    // Element Count: 65, Action Count: 11
    // Largest Action Index: 4 using 13 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, smCode), >=, 11));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, smSpecial, Player Variable(Event Player, smCode));
        Else If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, smCode), >=, 6));
            If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, smHolding), ==, False));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 31, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 111), Subtract(Player Variable(Event Player, smCode), 6)));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 32, Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 30));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, smHolding, True);
            End;
        Else If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, smCode), >=, 1));
            Press Button(Event Player, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 111), Subtract(Player Variable(Event Player, smCode), 1)));
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 48
rule("wave boss: find my row")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 5, Condition Count: 1
    conditions
    {
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 42, Action Count: 6
    // Largest Action Index: 0 using 18 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, smRow, If-Then-Else(Array Contains(Global Variable(bossBots), Event Player), 0, Add(Index Of Array Value(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 110), Hero Of(Event Player)), 1)));
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, smRow), >, 0));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, smCode, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 113), Subtract(Player Variable(Event Player, smRow), 1)));
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 30, 0);
            Call Subroutine(SmDispatch);
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 135
rule("wave boss: timed sequence")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 12, Condition Count: 2
    conditions
    {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Player Variable(Event Player, smRow) > 0;
    }

    // Element Count: 122, Action Count: 13
    // Largest Action Index: 4 using 49 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 28, 0);
        While(Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 28), <, 6));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, smEntry, Multiply(Add(Multiply(Subtract(Player Variable(Event Player, smRow), 1), 6), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 28)), 5));
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 29, Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 10));
            If(And(And(Compare(Global Variable(Difficulty), >=, Value In Array(Global Variable(smActions), Add(Player Variable(Event Player, smEntry), 4))), Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 29), >=, Value In Array(Global Variable(smActions), Add(Player Variable(Event Player, smEntry), 2)))), Compare(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 29), <, Value In Array(Global Variable(smActions), Add(Player Variable(Event Player, smEntry), 3)))));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, smCode, Value In Array(Global Variable(smActions), Player Variable(Event Player, smEntry)));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 30, Value In Array(Global Variable(smActions), Add(Player Variable(Event Player, smEntry), 1)));
                Call Subroutine(SmDispatch);
            End;
            Wait(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 114), Subtract(Global Variable(Difficulty), 1)), Abort When False);
            Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 28, Add, 1);
        End;
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 30, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 112), Add(Multiply(Subtract(Player Variable(Event Player, smRow), 1), 3), 2)));
        Call Subroutine(SmDispatch);
    }
}

        Set Global Variable(decodeIn, Value In Array(Array(Array(1, 1, -180, Array(Custom String("̶̶̶̶̶̶̳̹̬̳˶̷̶̴̷̶̷̶̶̶̶̶̴̷̳̺̳̹̳̹̳̹̳̳̳͒̽͜՞̶̶̳՟̸̶̶̶̵̵̳̳̳ͥ̀˕̸̷̶̶̳̳̃՛̶̶̴̶̶̶̶̳̳#̴̶̶̶̶̷̴̶̶̶̶$̴̶̶̷̻˘̴̷̶̷̶̶̸̷̻̹̳̹̳՞̸̳̀,̶̶̶̳̥̳́׽̷̳̽֋̷̳̹!"), Custom String("̷̳̹׾̷̳̺׾̶̶̳̶̶̶ׁ̳̳̻$̸̴̷̵̴̶̶̳͓̼̽̽̈́ͦ͆#̶̶̳%̳"))), Array(1, 1, -179, Array(Custom String("̵̵̶̵̵̵̵֢̲̻֣̲֢̲։̵̲̹։̵̶̵̵̵̳̹̲͆~̸̵̲˩̷̵̲՝̵̵̲˒̵̲̻!̵̵̲&̵̵̲։̶̵̲˅̷̵̲՘̶̵̲˙̶̶̲՟̷̵̲԰̷̶̳̻̺̳͍̺̓֔͜՜̸̶̳͗̈́֋̳̽͏̵̶˓̶̶̲̺̲̻֘«̷̵̲˅̵̵̲՝̵̶̲՘̵̶̲͹̵̵̲΅"), Custom String("̵̲̺«̵̶̲©̵̵̷̵̲̲̚΂̷̵̶̳̻֎̶̳̻"))), Array(1, 1, -180, Array(Custom String("̷ְ̻̳̻̾΢̷̷̷̶̳̻̲̳̹͕̳׽̷̶̳!̴̷̶̷̺՟̵̷̵̷ֶ̳̳̶̳̹.̶̳̹׬̸̶̳։̶̳̹՚̴̷̷̵̶͕͇̺̳̺̳ͨ̇՜̷̳̺՞̵̶̳՞̸̳̹֍̸̸̳՟̷̷̳$̵̷̳˄̷̳̺˄̵̷̳˄̷̳̻˗̷̶̳׽̴̷̶̷̶#̷̳̽˪̷̸̷̳̺̳̹̖"), Custom String("̷̳̼̷̳̺#̷̳̺׾̸̷̳¿̴̷̶͔͉×̴͔͎")))), Global Variable(telespot)));

        Call Subroutine(decompressNSub);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 57, Global Variable(decodeOut));
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 298
rule("build the zone's doors")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        BuildZoneDoors;
    }

    // Element Count: 297, Action Count: 51
    // Largest Action Index: 33 using 33 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 2, 0);
        For Global Variable(doorBuildIndex, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner)), 1);
            Set Global Variable(doorIndex, Global Variable(doorBuildIndex));
            Call Subroutine(DestroyDoorEffects);
        End;
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), active_wall, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 3, Value In Array(Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 71), Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 73), Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 75)), Global Variable(telespot)));
        Call Subroutine(LoadZoneDoorData);
        Set Global Variable(doorBuildIndex, 0);
        Set Global Variable(looping, 0);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Corner, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_OppositeCorner, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Center, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Facing, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Side, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Width, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_State, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Zone, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_BeamSpan, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_BeamA, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_BeamB, Empty Array);
        While(Compare(Global Variable(doorBuildIndex), <, Count Of(Global Variable(doorVectors))));
            If(Not(Modulo(Global Variable(doorBuildIndex), 2)));
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            End;
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Corner, Append To Array, Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(doorVectors), Global Variable(doorBuildIndex))));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_OppositeCorner, Append To Array, Array(Null));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Center, Append To Array, Array(Null));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Facing, Append To Array, Array(Direction From Angles(Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(doorParams), Add(Global Variable(looping), 2)), 180), 0)));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Side, Append To Array, Array(Direction From Angles(Subtract(Value In Array(Global Variable(doorParams), Add(Global Variable(looping), 2)), 90), 0)));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Width, Append To Array, Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(doorParams), Global Variable(looping))));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_State, Append To Array, Array(If-Then-Else(Array Contains(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 56), Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 3), Global Variable(doorBuildIndex))), 0, 1)));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Zone, Append To Array, Array(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 57), Global Variable(doorBuildIndex))));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_BeamSpan, Append To Array, Array(Add(Multiply(Direction From Angles(Subtract(Value In Array(Global Variable(doorParams), Add(Global Variable(looping), 2)), 90), 0), Value In Array(Global Variable(doorParams), Global Variable(looping))), Multiply(Up, Value In Array(Global Variable(doorParams), Add(Global Variable(looping), 1))))));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_BeamA, Append To Array, Array(Null));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_BeamB, Append To Array, Array(Null));
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(doorParams), Add(Global Variable(looping), 3)), ==, 1));
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_Width, Global Variable(doorBuildIndex), Value In Array(Global Variable(doorParams), Add(Global Variable(looping), 4)));
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global Variable(doorBuildIndex), Add(Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(doorVectors), Global Variable(doorBuildIndex)), Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Side), Global Variable(doorBuildIndex)), Value In Array(Global Variable(doorParams), Add(Global Variable(looping), 4)))), Multiply(Up, Value In Array(Global Variable(doorParams), Add(Global Variable(looping), 5)))));
                Modify Global Variable(looping, Add, 6);
            Else;
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global Variable(doorBuildIndex), Add(Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(doorVectors), Global Variable(doorBuildIndex)), Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Side), Global Variable(doorBuildIndex)), Value In Array(Global Variable(doorParams), Global Variable(looping)))), Vector(Empty Array, 50, Empty Array)));
                Modify Global Variable(looping, Add, 4);
            End;
            Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_Center, Global Variable(doorBuildIndex), Divide(Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_Corner), Global Variable(doorBuildIndex)), Value In Array(Global Variable(allDoors_OppositeCorner), Global Variable(doorBuildIndex))), 2));
            Modify Global Variable(doorBuildIndex, Add, 1);
        End;
        Set Global Variable(doorVectors, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(doorParams, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 57, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 2, 1);
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 307
rule("zone loading: set up the team's zone")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 14, Condition Count: 2
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(mapDataLoaded) == True;
        Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 134) != Global Variable(telespot);
    }

    // Element Count: 292, Action Count: 34
    // Largest Action Index: 26 using 68 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Global Variable(zoneLoading, True);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 134, Global Variable(telespot));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 138, And(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 8), !=, Null), Compare(Is Alive(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 8)), ==, True)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 139, 0);
        While(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 139), <, 12));
            If(Compare(Players In Slot(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 139), Team 2), !=, Null));
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 139));
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            End;
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 139, Add, 1);
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 136, True);
        Set Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(averagePosition, Null);
        Call Subroutine(RebuildBreachEffects);
        Call Subroutine(BuildZoneDoors);
        Wait(1.5, Ignore Condition);
        Wait Until(And(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 135), ==, False), Compare(Global Variable(doorsNeedDrawing), ==, False)), 30);
        Set Global Variable(pfMapReady, False);
        Set Global Variable(pfDecompress, 0);
        Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 134), ==, 0));
            Set Global Variable(pfDecompress, Array(Array(Custom String("޴୐޼ૼ޵׋޳̶޼૷޴૱ް੟޼ૢ޵˷ްֻ޼ࡿ޶ُ޲̕޼ࢀ޶ા޳ֵ޼ࡿ޶¿޵̊޼ଁ޶̔޵׋޼૥޶ி޵੔޼૥޷޵ૈ޼૥޷࣎޳ெ޼ࢁ޶࢔޲૚޼ु޷ͤ޳̃޼ु޷ੇްً޼޳޷ؚް޼ޯ޸ް|޼ु޸॥ް޶޼ु޸੐ް؀޼਺޻҈޲̳޼৽޻௎޲৅޼਒޼ࣳ޳޼৽޻ோ"), Custom String("޲৉޼਒޻؎ް׏޼਻޼૝ޯ॥޼਺޼¤ޯ੅޼਺޻࡫ް˹޼ੇ޻ிްী޻௎޼޴̌޼:޼]޴̨޼:޻ؑ޵̘޼:޻؍޵̔޼:޼ࠟ޴͢޼:޼ࢀ޴͈޼͆޺ࢎ޵ࠦ޼ޯ޺؎޶׵޼ޯ޺ொ޶׽޼ޯ޺׆޶঳޼ޯ޺΋޶࣢޼ޯ޹ؘ޵ۘ޼ޯ޹֎޵؄޼ޯ޹੍޳֤޼ޯ޹਀޳҃޼ޯ޸܊"), Custom String("޲ީ޼ޯ޸਻޲࡫޼ޯ޹੶޲؎޼ّ޺ࢎ޲ۘ޼֣޻ׄްؒ޻௎޻ִް؋޻௎޻਒ް˕޼ੇ޼ްِ޻௎޼΃ް~޼਺޻୾޵߾޼ࡿ޸͓޶ֹ޼ु޸՝޶֓޼஽޹϶޴֠޼ࡿ޷ீ޲՘޼ޯ޹;޲΅޼৙޹ૉް࠳޼਻޹ூްި޼਻޾˷޲֣޼৙޾̖޲֓޼ޯ޾਒ް޾޼ु޿͏޲ַ޼ޯ޿΢"), Custom String("޳΢޼ޯ޿֞޳͍޼ޯ޾͊޲࡚޼ޯ޾؊޵׶޼ޯ޾̼޵ޫ޼ޯ޽ޮ޴֪޼͆޽ؚ޳޼ّ޽࠮޲ٔ޼ّ޽߱޲۠޼֣޼ंް݈޻்޼৅޳͗޼ّ޺ࡴ޶֨޼ࠩ޻஻޴ࣶ޼ࠫ޻௒޶֙޼ޯ޽޶۟޼ޯ޽ঁ޶ુ޼ޯ޾̩޶̷޼ޯ޾঴޵۠޼ޯ޾ু޵܇޼ࡿ޿े޶ֲ޼ु޿ऺ޶׭޼஽޾ૈ"), Custom String("޴˅޼ࡿ߫«޴̔޼ࢀ߬਑޲઱޼ु߫ॗ޳˰޼ु͔߫޵̡޼૿߬઱޴૓޼૷߭ે޳΅޼૵߮̔ް੣޼૞͎߮ްֽ޼ࡿ߬ࢅ޲޼ࢁ̢߬ްؐ޼ޯؙ߫ް ޼ޯ޿૳ް޼ु޿ͦ޳֠޼ࡿ߬ૅ޵֧޼૥߬±޵੥޼૥߫ୀ޵૗޼૥߫֬޶־޻ெ޼ࣣ޶ࣿ޻௕޼ ޷ͦ޻଩޼#޶׵޻ி޻ٗ"), Custom String("޶؅޼ ޺਑޷࠷޼˜޺ं޷܋޼֣޹઻޶৓޼ޯ޹ૈ")), Array(Custom String("# (  ! $ '  # %  $ &  % ' /  & # ( ,  ' ! )  ( *  ) +  *  ' -   , .  -  & :  / ;  : <  ;    > _   = ? // Rule Element Count: 204
rule("zone loading: bring the zombies back")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 8, Condition Count: 1
    conditions
    {
        Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 137) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 195, Action Count: 19
    // Largest Action Index: 12 using 45 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 137, False);
        If(And(And(Compare(Global Variable(start), ==, 1), Compare(Global Variable(numberOfZombies), >=, 1)), Compare(Global Variable(Bosssequencer), ==, 0)));
            Call Subroutine(setbotamount);
            Set Global Variable(botamount, Min(Global Variable(botamount), Subtract(Global Variable(numberOfZombies), If-Then-Else(And(Compare(Global Variable(spawntype), !=, 4), Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 138), ==, True)), 1, 0))));
            If(And(And(Compare(Global Variable(spawntype), !=, 4), Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 138), ==, True)), Compare(Players In Slot(0, Team 2), ==, Null)));
                Create Dummy Bot(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 7), Global Variable(g)), Team 2, 0, First Of(Spawn Points(Team 2)), Subtract(Left, Left));
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 8, Players In Slot(0, Team 2));
                Wait(0.75, Ignore Condition);
            End;
            Set Global Variable(zoneRefillSlot, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(spawntype), ==, 4), 0, 1));
            While(And(Compare(Global Variable(zoneRefillSlot), <=, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(spawntype), ==, 4), Subtract(Global Variable(botamount), 1), Global Variable(botamount))), Compare(Global Variable(zoneLoading), ==, False)));
                If(Compare(Players In Slot(Global Variable(zoneRefillSlot), Team 2), ==, Null));
                    Create Dummy Bot(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(spawntype), ==, 4), Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Random Integer(0, 37)), If-Then-Else(Compare(Modulo(Global Variable(zoneRefillSlot), 2), ==, 1), Value In Array(Global Variable(bot1spawn), Global Variable(g)), Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 15), Global Variable(g)))), Team 2, Global Variable(zoneRefillSlot), First Of(Spawn Points(Team 2)), Subtract(Left, Left));
                    Set Global Variable At Index(omnicspawn, Global Variable(zoneRefillSlot), Global Variable(zoneRefillSlot));
                    Wait(0.75, Ignore Condition);
                End;
                Modify Global Variable(zoneRefillSlot, Add, 1);
            End;
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 175
rule("all the effects")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 6, Condition Count: 1
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(start) == 1;
    }

    // Element Count: 168, Action Count: 19
    // Largest Action Index: 2 using 18 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Global Variable At Index(buyperks, 8, Vector(240.047, 46.147, 174.277));
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 3, 1);
            Create Effect(Local Player, Good Aura, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 120), Value In Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Global Variable(telespot)), Evaluate Once(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 58), Global Variable(forindex))), Visible To Position Radius and Color);
            Create Effect(Local Player, Sparkles, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 120), Value In Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Global Variable(telespot)), Evaluate Once(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 58), Global Variable(forindex))), Visible To Position Radius and Color);
            Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(White), Value In Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Global Variable(telespot)), Evaluate Once(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 58), Global Variable(forindex))), Visible To Position Radius and Color);
        End;
        Wait(1, Ignore Condition);
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 3, 1);
            Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Red), Value In Array(Global Variable(Died), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(forindex))), 4, Visible To Position and Radius);
            Create Effect(Local Player, Sphere, Color(White), Value In Array(Global Variable(Died), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(forindex))), Player Variable(Players In Slot(Evaluate Once(Global Variable(forindex)), Team 1), sea), Visible To Position and Radius);
            Create In-World Text(Local Player, Custom String("{0}", Evaluate Once(Random Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 61)))), Value In Array(Global Variable(Died), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(forindex))), 3, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(White), Default Visibility);
        End;
        Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Purple), Global Variable(rngbox), 2, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Create Effect(Local Player, Light Shaft, Color(Sky Blue), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 10, Empty Array), Global Variable(rngbox)), 1, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Create Effect(Local Player, Light Shaft, Color(Sky Blue), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 25, Empty Array), Global Variable(rngbox)), 1, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Create Effect(Local Player, Light Shaft, Color(Sky Blue), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 40, Empty Array), Global Variable(rngbox)), 1, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Create In-World Text(Local Player, Icon String(Question Mark), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Global Variable(rngbox)), 2.25, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(Purple), Default Visibility);
        Create Effect(Local Player, Sparkles, Color(Yellow), Global Variable(rngbox), 2, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Symmetra Photon Projector, Null, Add(Up, Global Variable(rngbox)), Up, 0.1, Visible To Friendly To);
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 78
rule("clear zone markers")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        ClearZoneMarkers;
    }

    // Element Count: 77, Action Count: 22
    // Largest Action Index: 2 using 9 Elements
    actions
    {
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(zoneEffects)), 1);
            Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Global Variable(zoneEffects), Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)));
            If(Compare(Modulo(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex), 10), ==, 9));
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            End;
        End;
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(zoneTexts)), 1);
            Destroy In-World Text(Value In Array(Global Variable(zoneTexts), Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)));
            If(Compare(Modulo(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex), 10), ==, 9));
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            End;
        End;
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 129)), 1);
            Destroy Icon(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 129), Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)));
            If(Compare(Modulo(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex), 10), ==, 9));
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            End;
        End;
        Set Global Variable(zoneEffects, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(zoneTexts, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 129, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(powerconnected, Null);
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 606
rule("draw zone markers")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        DrawZoneMarkers;
    }

    // Element Count: 605, Action Count: 86
    // Largest Action Index: 56 using 52 Elements
    actions
    {
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, 7, 1);
            If(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), First Of(Sorted Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Distance Between(Value In Array(Global Variable(buyperks), Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global Variable(telespot)));
                Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Red), Value In Array(Global Variable(buyperks), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex))), 1, Visible To And Color);
                Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                If(Compare(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex), !=, 6));
                    Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Baptiste Biotic Launcher, Null, Value In Array(Global Variable(buyperks), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex))), Up, 0.1, Visible To Friendly To);
                    Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                    Create In-World Text(Local Player, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 143), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex))), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global Variable(buyperks), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)))), If-Then-Else(Compare(Evaluate Once(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)), ==, 0), 3.25, 2.25), Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(White), Default Visibility);
                    Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
                Else;
                    Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Zarya Graviton, Null, Add(Vector(Empty Array, 1.5, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global Variable(buyperks), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)))), Up, 0.05, Visible To Friendly To);
                    Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                    If(Compare(Global Variable(poweron), ==, 0));
                        Create Icon(All Living Players(Team 1), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global Variable(buyperks), 6)), Bolt, Visible To And Position, Color(Yellow), True);
                        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 129, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                        Set Global Variable(powerconnected, Last Created Entity);
                    End;
                End;
            End;
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        End;
        If(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), First Of(Sorted Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Distance Between(Value In Array(Global Variable(buyperks), 8), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global Variable(telespot)));
            Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Red), Value In Array(Global Variable(buyperks), 8), 1, Visible To);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Baptiste Biotic Launcher, Null, Value In Array(Global Variable(buyperks), 8), Up, 0.1, Visible To Friendly To);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            Create In-World Text(Local Player, Ability Icon String(Hero(Ashe), Button(Ability 2)), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global Variable(buyperks), 8)), 3.25, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To, Color(White), Default Visibility);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
        End;
        Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(buyheros)), 1);
            If(And(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), First Of(Sorted Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Distance Between(Value In Array(Global Variable(buyheros), Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global Variable(telespot)), Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)), !=, Null)));
                Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Yellow), Value In Array(Global Variable(buyheros), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex))), 2, Visible To);
                Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                Create In-World Text(Local Player, Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)))), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global Variable(buyheros), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)))), 2.25, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To, Color(Blue), Default Visibility);
                Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
            End;
            If(Compare(Modulo(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex), 6), ==, 5));
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            End;
        End;
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(bossbuypositions)), 1);
            If(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), First Of(Sorted Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Distance Between(Value In Array(Global Variable(bossbuypositions), Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global Variable(telespot)));
                Create In-World Text(Local Player, Custom String("Spawn boss: {0} : 15000 points", Input Binding String(Button(Interact))), Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(bossbuypositions), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex))), Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), 1, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To, Color(White), Default Visibility);
                Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
                Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(White), Value In Array(Global Variable(bossbuypositions), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex))), 1, Visible To);
                Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            End;
        End;
        Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        If(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), First Of(Sorted Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Distance Between(First Of(Global Variable(bankButtons)), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global Variable(telespot)));
            For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, 4, 1);
                Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Value In Array(Array(Color(Red), Color(Red), Color(Red), Color(Yellow)), Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)), Value In Array(Global Variable(bankButtons), Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)), Value In Array(Array(1.5, 1, 1, 1), Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)), Visible To);
                Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            End;
            For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, 4, 1);
                Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Baptiste Biotic Launcher, Null, Add(First Of(Global Variable(bankButtons)), Value In Array(Array(Vector(Empty Array, 0.25, 0.7), Vector(Empty Array, -0.5, Empty Array), Vector(0.5, -0.5, Empty Array), Vector(-0.5, -0.5, Empty Array)), Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex))), Value In Array(Array(Backward, Up, Up, Up), Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)), Value In Array(Array(0.05, 0.1, 0.1, 0.1), Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex)), Visible To Friendly To);
                Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            End;
            Create In-World Text(Local Player, Custom String("Property damage: ${0}", Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 47)), Value In Array(Global Variable(bankButtons), 3), 1, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(Yellow), Default Visibility);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);

            Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);

// Rule Element Count: 35
rule("zone markers")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 15, Condition Count: 2
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(start) == 1;
        Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 128) != Global Variable(telespot);
    }

    // Element Count: 19, Action Count: 7
    // Largest Action Index: 2 using 9 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 128, Global Variable(telespot));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 135, True);
        Wait Until(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 136), ==, True), 15);
        Call Subroutine(ClearZoneMarkers);
        Call Subroutine(DrawZoneMarkers);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 135, False);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 181
rule("fire sale boxes")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 11, Condition Count: 2
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(start) == 1;
        Global Variable(fireSale) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 169, Action Count: 28
    // Largest Action Index: 8 using 28 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 130, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 131, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(fireSaleBoxes, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 132, Global Variable(telespot));
        For Global Variable(fireSaleIndex, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(mysterylocations)), 1);
            If(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), First Of(Sorted Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Distance Between(Value In Array(Global Variable(mysterylocations), Global Variable(fireSaleIndex)), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global Variable(telespot)));
                Modify Global Variable(fireSaleBoxes, Append To Array, Value In Array(Global Variable(mysterylocations), Global Variable(fireSaleIndex)));
            End;
            If(And(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), First Of(Sorted Array(Global Variable(teleporterpositions), Distance Between(Value In Array(Global Variable(mysterylocations), Global Variable(fireSaleIndex)), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global Variable(telespot)), Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(mysterylocations), Global Variable(fireSaleIndex)), !=, Global Variable(rngbox))));
                Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Purple), Value In Array(Global Variable(mysterylocations), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(fireSaleIndex))), 2, Visible To);
                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 130, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                Create Effect(Local Player, Light Shaft, Color(Orange), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 10, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global Variable(mysterylocations), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(fireSaleIndex)))), 1, Visible To);
                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 130, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                Create In-World Text(Local Player, Icon String(Question Mark), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global Variable(mysterylocations), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(fireSaleIndex)))), 2.25, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To, Color(Orange), Default Visibility);
                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 131, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
            End;
        End;
        Wait Until(Or(Compare(Global Variable(fireSale), !=, True), Compare(Global Variable(telespot), !=, Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 132))), 99999);
        For Global Variable(fireSaleIndex, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 130)), 1);
            Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 130), Global Variable(fireSaleIndex)));
        End;
        For Global Variable(fireSaleIndex, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 131)), 1);
            Destroy In-World Text(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 131), Global Variable(fireSaleIndex)));
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 130, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 131, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(fireSaleBoxes, Empty Array);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

    }
}

    event
    {

        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 22, Condition Count: 2
    conditions

        Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 18) == False;
        Is True For Any(Global Variable(buyheros), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 96, Action Count: 11
    // Largest Action Index: 4 using 58 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);

            If(And(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global Variable(buyheros), Global Variable(i))), <, 2), Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Global Variable(i)), !=, Null)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot, Global Variable(i));
                Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} Press: {1} for Hero = {2}", Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global Variable(hero), Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot))), Input Binding String(Button(Interact)), Custom String("{0} Points -- Hold: {1} for follower = {2}", Round To Integer(Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(herobuycosts), Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot)), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), To Nearest), Input Binding String(Button(Reload)), Custom String("{0} Points --- Ammo cost {1}", Round To Integer(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(herobuycosts), Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot)), 3), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), To Nearest), Round To Integer(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global Variable(herobuycosts), Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot)), 2), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), To Nearest)))));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 18, True);
                Wait(5, Ignore Condition);
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 18, False);
                Break;
            End;
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 119
rule("Purchase perk machine message")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 26, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Player Variable(Event Player, perkBuyMessTimer) == False;
        Player Variable(Event Player, outofpsawn) == 1;
        Is True For Any(Global Variable(buyperks), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 92, Action Count: 17
    // Largest Action Index: 9 using 30 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
        For Global Variable(i, 0, 7, 1);
            If(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global Variable(buyperks), Global Variable(i))), <, 2));
                If(And(Compare(Global Variable(poweron), ==, 0), Compare(Global Variable(i), ==, 6)));
                    Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} Dont you dare touch that!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
                Else If(Compare(Global Variable(poweron), ==, 0));
                    Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} What a Mysterious Machine for my Trials!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
                Else If(Compare(Global Variable(i), !=, 6));
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot, Global Variable(i));
                    Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} Press: {1} for Perk = {2}", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)), Input Binding String(Button(Interact)), Custom String("{0} == {1} Points", Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 142), Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot)), Round To Integer(Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(perkcosts), Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot)), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), To Nearest))));
                End;
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, perkBuyMessTimer, True);
                Wait(5, Ignore Condition);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, perkBuyMessTimer, False);
                Break;
            End;
        End;
    }
}

    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 6, Condition Count: 1
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(start) > 0;
    }

    // Element Count: 42, Action Count: 7
    // Largest Action Index: 1 using 11 Elements
    actions
    {

        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, InfoUI, Remove From Array By Index, Subtract(Count Of(Player Variable(Event Player, InfoUI)), 1));
        Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
        Loop If(Compare(Count Of(Player Variable(Event Player, InfoUI)), !=, 0));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 51, Add(Total Time Elapsed, 5));
        Abort;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 139
rule("Info UI")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 5, Condition Count: 1
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(start) == False;
    }

    // Element Count: 133, Action Count: 16
    // Largest Action Index: 5 using 50 Elements
    actions
    {
        For Player Variable(Event Player, infoUIIndex, 0, 18, 1);
            If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, infoUIIndex), >, 0));
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            End;
            If(Array Contains(Array(0, 7, 10, 14), Player Variable(Event Player, infoUIIndex)));
                Create HUD Text(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(settingsViewers), ==, Null), Event Player, Null), Null, Null, Custom String("{0} {1}", Evaluate Once(Value In Array(Array(Icon String(Checkmark), Icon String(Bolt), Icon String(Diamond), Icon String(Heart)), Index Of Array Value(Array(0, 7, 10, 14), Player Variable(Event Player, infoUIIndex)))), Value In Array(Global Variable(infoText), Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, infoUIIndex)))), Left, Add(10, Player Variable(Event Player, infoUIIndex)), Null, Null, Color(Yellow), Visible To And String, Default Visibility);
            Else If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, infoUIIndex), ==, 5));
                Create HUD Text(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(settingsViewers), ==, Null), Event Player, Null), Null, Null, Value In Array(Global Variable(infoText), 5), Left, 15, Null, Null, Color(Green), Visible To And String, Default Visibility);
            Else;
                Create HUD Text(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(settingsViewers), ==, Null), Event Player, Null), Null, Value In Array(Global Variable(infoText), Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, infoUIIndex))), Null, Left, Add(10, Player Variable(Event Player, infoUIIndex)), Null, Color(White), Null, Visible To And String, Default Visibility);
            End;
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, InfoUI, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
        End;
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 51, Add(Total Time Elapsed, 0.16));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 49, True);
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 35, 1);
    }
}

    }

rule("settings menu: show it")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 15, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(start) == False;
        Global Variable(textListsLoaded) == True;
        Global Variable(mapDataLoaded) == True;

    // Element Count: 171, Action Count: 11
    // Largest Action Index: 4 using 123 Elements
    actions
    {
        Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        Create In-World Text(Global Variable(settingsViewers), Custom String("+"), Add(First Of(Global Variable(settingsCamera)), Multiply(Facing Direction Of(Local Player), 12)), 3, Do Not Clip, Visible To Position and String, Color(Yellow), Default Visibility);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 133, Array(Last Text ID));
        For Global Variable(settingsRow, 0, 9, 1);
            Create In-World Text(Global Variable(settingsViewers), Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 10), Value In Array(Array(0, 1, Add(3, Global Variable(Difficulty)), Add(9, Global Variable(spawntype)), Add(14, If-Then-Else(Global Variable(experiencesystem), 1, 0)), 2, Add(14, Multiply(2, Global Variable(Difficulty))), Add(15, Multiply(2, Global Variable(Difficulty))), 3), Evaluate Once(Global Variable(settingsRow)))), Add(Value In Array(Global Variable(settingsCamera), 1), Multiply(Value In Array(Global Variable(settingsCamera), 2), Subtract(3, Multiply(Global Variable(settingsRow), 0.75)))), If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(settingsRow), ==, 0), 3, If-Then-Else(Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Global Variable(settingsRow), 3.5)), <, 2), 2.2, 1.3)), Do Not Clip, Visible To And String, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global Variable(settingsRow), ==, 0), Color(Yellow), If-Then-Else(Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Global Variable(settingsRow), 3.5)), <, 2), Color(Orange), Color(White))), Default Visibility);
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 133, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
        End;
        Wait Until(Compare(Global Variable(start), !=, False), 99999);
        For Global Variable(settingsRow, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 133)), 1);
            Destroy In-World Text(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 133), Global Variable(settingsRow)));
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 128
rule("settings menu: click a setting")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 17, Condition Count: 2
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(settingsViewers) != Null;
        Or(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)), Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire))) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 110, Action Count: 14
    // Largest Action Index: 1 using 16 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 42, If-Then-Else(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)), 1, -1));
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, menuPick, Round To Integer(Subtract(2, Multiply(16, Dot Product(Facing Direction Of(Event Player), Value In Array(Global Variable(settingsCamera), 2)))), To Nearest));
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, menuPick), ==, 0));
            Set Global Variable(Difficulty, Max(1, Min(5, Add(Global Variable(Difficulty), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 42)))));
            Set Global Variable(difficultyStats, Array Slice(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 30), Subtract(Multiply(Global Variable(Difficulty), 6), 6), 6));
        Else If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, menuPick), ==, 1));
            Set Global Variable(spawntype, Modulo(Add(Add(Global Variable(spawntype), Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection), 42)), 5), 5));
        Else If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, menuPick), ==, 2));
            Set Global Variable(experiencesystem, Not(Global Variable(experiencesystem)));
        Else If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, menuPick), ==, 3));
            Set Global Variable(settingsViewers, Null);
        End;
        Play Effect(Event Player, Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 40);
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
    }
}

            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Sombra Translocator Reappear Sound, First Of(All Players(All Teams)), Eye Position(First Of(All Players(All Teams))), 100);
        End;
        Wait(If-Then-Else(Array Contains(Array(Custom String("."), Custom String("!"), Custom String("?"), Custom String("-")), Char In String(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 123), Global Variable(Character_Nr))), 0.35, 0.05), Ignore Condition);
        Wait Until(Compare(Global Variable(Character_Nr), <=, String Length(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 123))), 99999);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 115
rule("boss buy")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 20, Condition Count: 2
    conditions
    {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Is True For Any(Global Variable(bossbuypositions), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 94, Action Count: 14
    // Largest Action Index: 3 using 23 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        If(Compare(Global Variable(Bosssequencer), !=, 0));
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} One champion at a time!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
        Else If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, points), >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(15000, Raise To Power(0.5, Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses))), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), Player Variable(Event Player, creditcardlimit)), To Nearest)));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Multiply(15000, Raise To Power(0.5, Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses))), Global Variable(costmultiplier)), Player Variable(Event Player, cashback)), To Nearest));
            Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, 1);
            Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins);
            Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, points)), 15000, Destination And Rate);
            Play Effect(All Players(Team 1), Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70);
            Set Global Variable(bossSpot, First Of(Sorted Array(Global Variable(bossbuypositions), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element))));
            Set Global Variable(Bosssequencer, Add(Index Of Array Value(Global Variable(bossbuypositions), Global Variable(bossSpot)), 1));
        Else;
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} You cannot afford to face my champion!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 57
rule("spawn boss parts")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        SpawnBossParts;
    }

    // Element Count: 56, Action Count: 10
    // Largest Action Index: 6 using 14 Elements
    actions
    {
        For Global Variable(bossIndex, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(bossPartList)), 1);
            Create Dummy Bot(First Of(Value In Array(Global Variable(bossPartList), Global Variable(bossIndex))), Team 2, -1, Global Variable(bossSpot), Subtract(Left, Left));
            Modify Global Variable(bossBots, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            Set Player Variable(Last Of(Global Variable(bossBots)), Part, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(bossPartList), Global Variable(bossIndex)), 2));
            Start Scaling Player(Last Of(Global Variable(bossBots)), 0.7, True);
            Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
            Attach Players(Last Of(Global Variable(bossBots)), Global Variable(bossBody), Multiply(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global Variable(bossPartList), Global Variable(bossIndex)), 1), 0.7));
            Start Facing(Last Of(Global Variable(bossBots)), Facing Direction Of(Global Variable(bossBody)), 1000, To World, Direction And Turn Rate);
            Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
        End;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 81
rule("boss fight: clear the wave, then the boss comes")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }

    // Element Count: 6, Condition Count: 1
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(Bosssequencer) != 0;
    }

    // Element Count: 74, Action Count: 21
    // Largest Action Index: 15 using 21 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait Until(Compare(Value In Array(Global Variable(_extendedGlobalCollection), 28), ==, False), 2);
        Set Global Variable(numberOfZombies, 1);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 8, Null);
        Set Global Variable(bossBots, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(bossEffects, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(bossIndex, 0, 12, 1);
            If(Compare(Players In Slot(Global Variable(bossIndex), Team 2), !=, Null));
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Global Variable(bossIndex));
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            End;
        End;
        Big Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("A champion approaches!"));
        Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("{0} You want a real fight? Face my champion!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
        Wait(2, Abort When False);
        Skip(Value In Array(Array(5, 0, 2, 4), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(1, 2, 3), Global Variable(Bosssequencer)), 1)));
        Call Subroutine(SpawnDevastator);
        Skip(3);
        Call Subroutine(SpawnColossalCharger);
        Skip(1);
        Call Subroutine(SpawnImperialWarmech);
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 42
rule("boss fight: the boss attacks players in range")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }

    // Element Count: 16, Condition Count: 3
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(Bosssequencer) != 0;
        Event Player == Global Variable(bossBody);
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 25, Action Count: 7
    // Largest Action Index: 0 using 16 Elements
    actions
    {
        If(And(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget), !=, Null), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget)), <, 25)));
            Start Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire));
        Else;
            Stop Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire));
        End;
        Wait(0.5, Abort When False);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

// Rule Element Count: 37
rule("end boss fight")
{
    event
    {
        Subroutine;
        EndBossFight;
    }

    // Element Count: 36, Action Count: 14
    // Largest Action Index: 1 using 8 Elements
    actions
    {
        For Global Variable(bossIndex, 0, 12, 1);
            If(Compare(Players In Slot(Global Variable(bossIndex), Team 2), !=, Null));
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Global Variable(bossIndex));
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            End;
        End;
        For Global Variable(bossIndex, 0, Count Of(Global Variable(bossEffects)), 1);
            Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Global Variable(bossEffects), Global Variable(bossIndex)));
        End;
        Set Global Variable(bossEffects, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(bossBots, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(bossBody, Null);
        Set Global Variable(Bosssequencer, 0);
    }
}

    }

        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Evaluate Once(Player Variable(Event Player, points)), 20000, Destination And Rate);
    }
}

    // Element Count: 8, Action Count: 4

// Rule Element Count: 189
rule("289 Imperial warmech set attacks")
{
    event
    {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        D.Va;
    }

    // Element Count: 23, Condition Count: 4
    conditions
    {
        Global Variable(Bosssequencer) == 3;
        Event Player == Global Variable(bossBody);
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)) == True;
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
    }

    // Element Count: 165, Action Count: 25
    // Largest Action Index: 7 using 48 Elements
    actions
    {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, Major_Attacks, Random Integer(2, 6));
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
        If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, Major_Attacks), ==, 2));
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Wrecking Ball Piledriver Impact Sound, Color(White), Eye Position(Event Player), 200);
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Wrecking Ball Piledriver Impact Effect, Color(White), Position Of(Event Player), 1);
            Wait(0.75, Ignore Condition);
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, warmachAttackParts, Array(Array(Hero(Pharah), Vector(Empty Array, 5, Empty Array)), Array(Hero(Zarya), Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), Array(Hero(Sojourn), Vector(Empty Array, 5, Empty Array)), Array(Hero(Moira), Vector(Empty Array, 4, Empty Array)), Array(Hero(Junkrat), Vector(Empty Array, 4, -1))));
        For Player Variable(Event Player, warmachAttackIndex, 0, Count Of(Player Variable(Event Player, warmachAttackParts)), 1);
            If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, warmachAttackIndex), ==, Subtract(Player Variable(Event Player, Major_Attacks), 2)));
                Create Dummy Bot(First Of(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, warmachAttackParts), Player Variable(Event Player, warmachAttackIndex))), Team 2, -1, Event Player, Subtract(Left, Left));
                Modify Global Variable(bossBots, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
                Set Status(Last Of(Global Variable(bossBots)), Null, Phased Out, 9999);
                Start Scaling Player(Last Of(Global Variable(bossBots)), 0.7, True);
                Attach Players(Last Of(Global Variable(bossBots)), Event Player, Multiply(Value In Array(Value In Array(Player Variable(Event Player, warmachAttackParts), Player Variable(Event Player, warmachAttackIndex)), 1), 0.7));
                If(Compare(Player Variable(Event Player, Major_Attacks), ==, 3));
                    Start Facing(Last Of(Global Variable(bossBots)), Add(Facing Direction Of(Event Player), Vector(Random Integer(-1, 1), Empty Array, Empty Array)), 1000, To Player, Direction And Turn Rate);
                Else;
                    Start Facing(Last Of(Global Variable(bossBots)), Facing Direction Of(Event Player), 1000, To World, Direction And Turn Rate);
                End;
                Wait(7, Ignore Condition);
                Loop If Condition Is True;
            End;
        End;
    }
}

        Global Variable(Bosssequencer) == 3;

    }

    // Element Count: 44, Action Count: 7
    // Largest Action Index: 3 using 23 Elements
    actions
    {
        Wait(0.1, Abort When False);
        Kill(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 7, Team Of(Event Player), Off), Null);
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bastion Tank Cannon Explosion Sound, Color(White), Eye Position(Event Player), 200);
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bastion Tank Cannon Explosion Effect, Color(White), Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Vector(Random Integer(-4, 4), Random Integer(-3, 3), Random Integer(-3, 3))), 1);
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
        Wait Until(Is Dead(Event Player), 99999);
        Clear Status(Event Player, Burning);
    }
}
